### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 澳洲央行決議如預期升息一碼至 4.60%為今年第四度升息，基準利率創 2011 年
以來新高，澳洲央行在政策聲明中表示通膨壓力尚未緩解，不排除在11、12 月
政策會議進一步升息；另一方面，澳洲8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)月增 0.4%略低
於預期，但年增 4.0%仍高於澳洲央行 2~3%的目標區間。
⚫ 美國第二季美 GDP 終值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%， 其中實質個人消費支出創下2025
年 3 月以來最大單月增幅，顯示即使高利率持續，美國家庭消費依然具韌性；
另一方面，美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增月增 0.2%、3.0%，雙雙低於市場預
期的 0.3%、3.3%，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人，失
業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，8 月非農就業人數亦同步下修至 13.3 萬人，通膨下滑
和勞動力市場略有降溫，降低市場對聯準會 10 月升息預期，Fedwatch 顯示 10

月升息機率下降至 20%左右。
⚫ 受美伊衝突引發的能源價格衝擊影響 ，9 月歐元地區消費者物價調和指數(HICP)
整體上升速度遠超預期，其中，法國年增 3.4%、德國年增 3.3%，歐元區年增
3.8%，均高於歐洲央行的 2%目標，反映能源價格的衝擊持續影響物價，加深歐
洲央行再次升息的壓力。
⚫ 日本上週公佈貨幣政策會議紀要及央行意見摘要顯示多名委員認為當前政策利
率依然處於中性區間之下 ，需警惕通膨上行風險 ， 且未來應該以更快速度升息抑
制通膨，然而意見摘要提及日本經濟財政大臣城內實表示希望央行充分審視過
去升息對景氣之影響 ，市場解讀為政府牽制日本央行未來升息步伐 ， 而財務大臣
片山皋月則重申尊重日本央行的自主性。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.760，低點在 31.946，以 31.830 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 5 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶，週初，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方，市場預期聯準
會後續仍有升息的空間，提振美元指數表現，加以外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣走貶
至 31.900 上方；不過適逢月底也是季底，匯價逢高吸引出口商進場賣美金，緩解台
幣貶值的速度；臨近週末，美國 8 月核心 PCE 數據 3%低於預期 3.3%，但市場等待
9 月非農就業數據，交投偏向謹慎觀望，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.830。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略有
減弱，市場對於 10 月升息的預期下降，但仍預期 12 月會再度升息。美元指數雖然
脫離高點，但仍在 102 附近高位震盪，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，
台股再創收盤新高且外資轉為買超台股，且隨著 AI 需求持續帶動台灣經濟出口表
現強勁，預計出口商後續拋匯需求將持續提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，預估本週台幣
區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/5/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪偏升。隨著美中兩國完成新一輪談判，雙方同意延長休兵，從
原定的 11 月延長到明年 1 月 ， 降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險 ， 支撐人民幣的走勢由
6.7170 走升至 6.7030；不過由於美伊戰事持續 ，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方 ，
加以美元指數及美債收益率雙雙走高 ， 令人民幣由升轉貶至6.7200 上方 ； 臨近週末，
美國公布 9 月非農數據降溫，聯準會10 月升息預期下滑，美元指數回落，境外人民
幣走升至 6.7000 週內高點，終場境外人民幣收在 6.7061，週線升值 166 點，境內人
民幣於十一長假前夕收盤於在 6.7053，週線升值 72 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週人民幣區間震盪 。 就國際市場來看 ， 美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，遠低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略
為減弱。數據公布後，市場對於聯準會10 月升息的預期下降，美元指數小幅回落，
有助於人民幣的表現；就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價格再度升破 6.7400，有助
於人民幣的走勢 ， 不過10/1~10/7 是中國十一長假 ， 預計人民幣走勢主要將受國際美
元影響；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線仍向下發散，空頭格
局不變，不過匯價反彈至 20 日均線附近，顯示下行動能暫緩。綜合以上，預估本週
人民幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7300

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 震盪上行。週初，美元指數持穩在 101 以上高位，USD/JPY 大致
在 156.50 至 157.85 間波動；週間，適逢日本上半年會計年度結算，出口商拋匯使資
金匯回力道強勁 ， 帶動USD/JPY 下行最低來到 156.35，但逢低有買盤進場，USD/JPY
回到 157 整數關卡之上，後隨美債殖利率創高、美元指數突破 102 關卡，USD/JPY
上行最高來至 158.45；臨近週末，美國公布 9 月非農就業人數低於預期，USD/JPY
一度回落至 157 下方，終場收在 157.83，週線上漲 57 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪 ， 主要原因如下 ： 一 、日本 9 月製造業 PMI 由 54.9
回落至 54.1，增長速度放慢至六個月以來的低位，主因為產出和新訂單增長放緩，
經濟復甦力道可能減緩，不利日圓表現。二、美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.0%，
美國 9 月非農數據 2.9 萬人均低於預期，通膨和勞動力市場略有降溫使市場對聯準
會升息預期下滑，加之部分聯準會官員表示無需急於進一步升息，Fedwatch 顯示 10
月升息機率降至兩成，然而美國第二季 GDP 上修至 2.2%經濟基本面穩健仍將支撐
美元。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價處於區間波動，且各天期均線
趨平，上方 158.50 有年線、季線壓力，下方 156.25 則有月線支撐，預期短線以區間
震盪可能性較高。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.50-159.00 
前週交易區間 
156.35 158.45

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美元指數持穩在 101 以上高位，EUR/USD 震盪下
行至 1.1310，而在紐約聯儲主席威廉姆斯發表不急於再次升息的偏鴿言論後，
EUR/USD 反彈至 1.1340；週間，美國 8 月 PCE 數據低於預期，EUR/USD 一度反彈
至 1.1380，然而在全球債券拋售潮下，資金回流美元帶動美元指數突破 102 關卡，
EUR/USD 跌破 1.1300 關卡最低來至 1.1214；臨近週末，美國 9 月非農就業人數低
於預期，EUR/USD 反彈至 1.1285 後回落，終場收在 1.1253，週線下跌 138 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、上週公佈歐元地區消費者
物價調和指數(HICP)，法國年增 3.4%、德國年增 3.3%、歐元區年增 3.8%，顯示能
源價格衝擊依然使通膨壓力居高不下， 市場預期歐洲央行可能在 12 月再度升息 ， 將
帶給歐元部分支撐。二、美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.0%、月增 0.2%低於預
期，而 9 月非農就業人數 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人 ， 失業率則由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，
就業降溫及通膨回落降低市場對聯準會十月升息的預期，然而第二季美國 GDP 終
值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%，經濟基本面持穩仍有利美元維持強勢。三、技術面而言，在
日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價在各天期均線下方並跌破 1.1300 整數關卡，月線向下穿
過季線形成死亡交叉，顯示短線空方動能較強。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈
現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1150-1.1400 
前週交易區間 
1.1214 1.1391

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.29-115.10.02 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.65% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109     1.95% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.82%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 本週為提存期底 ， 繼央行上週大砍存單效應持續發酵 ， 市
場資金供給仍充裕，惟需求疲弱，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.66%~1.68%，利率高位較上週下跌 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投仍冷清，5 年期指標券 A15108 週三於處所成交在 1.82%，10 年
期指標券則未成交，週五等殖系統參考報價中價為 1.95%。 
第四季台債發行量雖偏少 ， 惟美伊和談依舊陷入僵局 ， 國際油價續處高位 ， 通
膨疑慮尚存，國際債市走勢仍偏空，預期台債殖利率短線將維持高檔盤整格
局，預估 5 年券區間為 1.85%~1.90%，10 年券區間為 1.95%~2%。

## Document 063: 2026年诺贝尔经济学奖点评：宏观稳定的微观基石

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年诺贝尔经济学奖点评：宏观稳定的微观基石.pdf
Report source: 工银国际证券
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

宏观经济深度研究 - 2026 年 10 月 13 日 1 
证券研究 
宏观经济深度研究 
宏观稳定的微观基石 
——2026 年诺贝尔经济学奖点评

“见微知著，睹始知终。”2026 年诺贝尔经济学奖花落罗伯特·霍尔与迈克尔·伍德
福德，以表彰两位学者为 “宏观经济稳定的微观基础 ”作出的开创性贡献。二人
的研究共同推动了宏观经济学从观察总量变化，走向理解总量背后的微观行为
机制。这在当前全球经济冲击来源日益复杂、周期性波动与结构性变化相互交
织的背景下，具有鲜明的现实意义。随着全球供应链重构、人口结构转变、地
缘政治风险以及技术变革等深层因素不断重塑经济运行模式，相似的总量数据
背后可能对应着截然不同的微观动因。因此，精准识别经济波动背后的微观行
为逻辑，并通过清晰、稳定且可信的政策信号引导家庭与企业形成良好的长期
预期，对提升宏观政策有效性愈发关键。这一理论也与当前中国宏观调控着力
扩大内需、稳定社会预期、增强政策取向一致性的导向高度契合。随着中国经
济体量持续扩大、金融市场不断深化以及市场主体日益多元，预期渠道在宏观
政策传导中的作用正日益凸显。从这一视角来看，稳预期既是稳定经济运行的
重要目标，也是强化宏观政策传导，进一步释放消费与投资潜力的重要机制。 
新晋诺奖获得者共同奠定了宏观稳定政策的微观基础。 2026 年诺贝尔经济学奖
授予罗伯特·霍尔（Robert E. Hall ）与迈克尔·伍德福德（Michael Woodford），以
表彰二人为 “宏观经济稳定的微观基础 ”作出的开创性贡献。霍尔现为斯坦福大
学经济学教授，曾于 2010 年担任美国经济学会主席，并长期担任美国国家经济
研究局（NBER）商业周期测定委员会主席。作为一位研究领域十分广泛的应用
宏观经济学家，霍尔长期关注消费、就业、通胀、技术、市场竞争以及宏观经
济政策。伍德福德现任哥伦比亚大学政治经济学教授，认知与行为经济学计划
主任，同时也是 NBER 行为宏观经济学工作组的创始联合主任。他的研究主要
集中于有限理性对经济分析的影响，在货币政策传导的微观基础、利率与通胀
决定、最优货币政策规则、中央银行沟通以及货币与财政政策互动等领域作出
了系统贡献。两人的共同贡献，在于推动宏观经济学从观察总量关系进一步走
向理解总量背后的微观行为机制，这促进了宏观稳定理论的范式转换，如今已
广泛应用于中央银行和宏观政策分析之中。如果说 2025 年诺贝尔经济学奖回答
了“经济增长从何而来”的长期命题，那么 2026 年的诺奖则将目光重新拉回经济
周期的另一面：当冲击无法避免，政策如何帮助经济恢复稳定？增长决定经济
能够走多远，稳定则决定经济如何穿越周期。霍尔与伍德福德从不同方向给出
了一个共同答案：宏观经济并不是独立于微观世界之外运行的庞然大物，消
费、就业、通胀与产出等总量指标，最终是无数家庭、劳动者和企业选择的集
合。霍尔将家庭消费从当期收入驱动的经验关系，重塑为基于未来预期的跨期
选择问题，揭示微观主体如何依据信息变化调整当前行为。伍德福德则将家庭
消费、企业定价、价格粘性与前瞻预期统一于现代货币政策框架，解释央行为
什么能够通过利率路径和预期管理稳定经济。从这一意义上看， 2026 年的诺奖
不仅是对两位宏观经济学家的肯定，也是对过去半个世纪宏观经济学演进方向
的一次总结：宏观结果由微观行为集聚而成，只有看见微观行为如何形成宏观
结果，宏观政策才能真正理解经济，并进一步稳定经济。 
从经验到预期，微观理解重塑消费与货币政策理论。 霍尔最具奠基意义的突破
来自消费理论。在传统凯恩斯函数中，家庭消费主要随着当期可支配收入变
化。而生命周期 —永久收入理论指出，家庭消费并不只取决于当期收入，还取
决于其对终身收入和财富的预期，并据此在不同阶段之间平滑消费。霍尔的核
心观点就在于，如果家庭已经利用所有已知信息决定当期消费，那么只有新的
信息才能改变其对未来收入的判断。但根据定义，新信息无法预测（否则它就

宏观经济深度研究 - 2026 年 10 月 13 日 2
证券研究
不是新信息了）。那么在一定前提条件下，消费的变化同样不应该被已有信息系
统性预测，因为它依赖于前瞻性家庭、理性预期等模型条件。在这一点上，霍
尔颠覆了传统的研究方法，他认为研究人员不应寻找能够预测家庭消费的指
标，而是应该检验 “消费变化无法预测 ”这一假设。微观研究给予了这一假设较
强支持，霍尔利用家庭层面数据发现，大约八成家庭的消费行为符合前瞻性消
费理论，其余家庭则更多受到当期收入和流动性条件约束。这一区别后来成为
现代宏观模型刻画不同家庭行为的重要基础。如果说霍尔更多解释了家庭和劳
动力市场如何形成宏观波动，那么伍德福德则系统回答了稳定政策如何通过这
些微观主体发挥作用。其研究将家庭跨期消费、企业最优定价、价格粘性和理
性预期纳入统一框架，推动现代新凯恩斯货币政策理论走向系统化。这一理论
最核心的贡献之一，是将霍尔的研究成果，即消费取决于家庭对其未来收入的
预期纳入模型。此外，伍德福德进一步将利率纳入家庭跨期消费决策框架，解

释央行如何通过改变实际利率影响消费与总需求。当实际利率高于自然利率
时，当前消费的相对成本上升，家庭倾向于推迟消费、增加储蓄，总需求随之
受到抑制；反之，当实际利率低于自然利率时，家庭更倾向于将未来消费提前
至当期，从而扩张总需求。因此，中央银行影响经济活动的关键，并不一定是
改变货币数量，也可以通过调整政策利率相对于自然利率的位置，改变家庭跨
期配置消费的选择。此外，伍德福德通过企业定价行为的处理进一步完善了这
一框架。在传统凯恩斯主义中，价格粘性长期被视为需求变化能够影响实际产
出和就业的重要原因，但为什么价格具有粘性、企业又如何决定调整价格，并
没有坚实的微观基础。伍德福德从单个企业的最优化决策出发解释这一问题：
现实中的企业调整价格存在成本，因此不会随着每一次需求和生产成本变化立
即重新定价；但一旦企业获得调整价格的机会，其决定又必然具有前瞻性。如
果企业预期未来自身成本上升、竞争对手价格提高或整体通胀走高，那么短期
就有动力设定更高的价格。当前通胀不只是当前供需状况的结果，也与企业对

未来经济环境的判断密切相关。在这一点上，家庭消费和企业定价具有一个共
同特征，即当下的行为依赖其对未来的预期。
预期如何放大冲击，也将如何稳定增长。 这套建立在微观行为与前瞻预期基础
上的框架，进一步改变了宏观政策识别冲击和稳定经济的方式。由于价格调整
存在摩擦，需求变化不会立即完全体现为价格变化，而会同时影响实际产出与
通胀。实际产出与价格充分灵活条件下 “自然产出”之间的差距，即产出缺口，
由此成为判断经济冷热和通胀压力的重要依据。这一框架也解释了为什么相似
的通胀表现可能需要不同的政策反应。如果通胀源于需求过度扩张，提高利率
可以压低消费和投资，使经济重新向自然产出靠拢；但如果通胀来自能源价格
上涨、供应链中断等负向供给冲击，经济本身能够实现的自然产出已经下降，
此时过度收紧需求反而可能进一步加剧产出损失。稳定政策因此首先需要识别
冲击究竟来自需求还是供给，再判断家庭消费、企业定价和经济活动将如何变
化。微观基础的价值正在于此：它不仅让模型更加严谨，也使政策制定从观察
表面的宏观数据进一步走向理解数据背后的行为机制。更重要的是，前瞻性意

味央行即使没有立即改变利率，只要能够可信地改变市场对未来政策路径的判
断，就可能提前影响消费、定价与通胀。货币政策由此从单纯的 “利率行动”进
一步走向“预期管理”，政策透明度、连续性与可信度也成为政策传导机制的重
要组成部分。正是在这里，霍尔与伍德福德的研究真正汇合：预期成为连接政
策、微观行为与宏观结果的关键纽带。政策影响预期，预期改变行为，行为塑
造经济，而新的经济结果又反过来影响政策。理解微观，是理解宏观的起点；
理解预期，则是理解现代宏观稳定政策如何真正发挥作用的关键。
对全球与中国的启示。 当前，全球经济正在进入一个宏观冲击来源更加多元的
时代，政策制定者除了要面对传统周期的扩张与收缩，还需要面对供应链、能
源、人口结构、地缘事件和人工智能等结构性冲击。在这一环境中，仅凭通
胀、就业和 GDP 等最终指标判断政策方向越来越困难，相同的数据表现可能由
完全不同的微观机制产生。 对于主要经济体而言， 在不确定性上升的环境中，

### Page 3

宏观经济深度研究 - 2026 年 10 月 13 日 3 
证券研究 
稳定政策预期有助于降低家庭和企业的观望情绪，也能够减少金融市场对于政
策路径的过度波动。 一方面，政策制定需要更加准确地区分冲击来源 。如果通
胀来自需求过热，提高利率能够通过抑制当前消费和投资缓解价格压力；但如
果通胀更多源于能源、供应链等供给冲击，过度压低需求反而可能带来更大的
产出损失。因此，宏观政策不能仅依据通胀率、失业率等最终结果机械调整，
而需要进一步判断家庭为何改变消费、企业为何调整价格，以及这些行为是短
期冲击还是长期预期变化所致。政策的有效性不只取决于力度，更取决于能否
准确作用于经济波动背后的微观机制。 另一方面，宏观政策需要更加重视可信
度和一致性。 霍尔和伍德福德的研究表明，预期管理本身正在成为宏观政策的
重要组成部分。家庭并不会简单依据当前收入决定消费，而会判断收入变化是
否具有持久性；企业也会根据未来利率、成本和价格水平决定短期的投资与定
价。政策连续性、透明度与可信度由此不只是沟通问题，更是决定政策传导效
率的重要宏观资源。清晰且可信的中长期政策路径，可以在政策落地之前就影
响消费、投资和价格。 对于中国而言， 随着经济规模扩大、金融市场深化和市
场主体日益多元，预期渠道正在成为宏观政策传导的重要组成部分。霍尔与伍
德福德的研究与当前宏观政策更加注重扩大内需、稳定预期和增强政策协同的
方向具有较强的现实契合度。2026 年《政府工作报告》强调增强政策的“前瞻性
针对性协同性”，着力“稳就业、稳企业、稳市场、稳预期 ”，同时提出增强宏观
政策取向一致性和有效性，并健全预期管理机制、提振社会信心。货币政策层
面，则进一步强调畅通货币政策传导机制，引导金融机构加力支持扩大内需等
重点领域。这一意义上看，随着预期管理在宏观政策中的重要性不断提升，稳
定就业和收入预期、增强政策协同并畅通传导机制，将有助于更好地将政策支
持转化为居民消费和企业投资动力，进一步释放内需潜力。

Initiation of Coverage
WinWay Technology
Final point of contact: securing AI yield through
advanced testing socket
Solid growth ahead with robust capacity expansions
We initiate coverage of AI/HPC test socket leader WinWay with a Buy rating and NT
$8,900 price target. WinWay's share price has corrected 43% from its April 2026 peak
due to broader market de-rating and margin pressure associated with its Technoprobe

partnership and capacity expansion. We believe the recent correction presents an
opportunity to re-engage with WinWay's long-term growth trajectory, supported by
three key drivers: 1) increasing content from AI GPUs, CPUs, and TPUs, 2) substantial
capacity expansion, and 3) broader adoption of system-level testing (SLT). Together,
these drivers should support revenue growth of 105%/74%/71% in 2026E/27E/28E.
Importantly, WinWay's growth is increasingly being driven by higher socket content per

device rather than semiconductor unit growth alone and the stock currently trades at
16x of our 2028E P/E vs historical average of 30x, potential to re-rate with GM recovery
in the coming quarters. See our companion testing deep dive sector note.
Rising yields and utilisation to offset probe card dilution
Gross margin declined to 38.3% in Q226 from 45.3% in FY25, pressured by outsourced
MEMS probes and expansion-related costs. While probe-card margins are unlikely to

return to H125 levels of 48.9% given the Technoprobe partnership structure, we expect
margin pressure to ease as utilization and yields improve, supporting GM recovery to
43.1% in 2027E and 44.7% in 2028E.
CPU and TPU as fundamental growth drivers
Beyond its core GPU socket franchise,  WinWay is well positioned to benefit from the
expansion of AI-oriented CPU and TPU platforms. We expect the company to supply
sockets for AMD's Venice CPU, Nvidia's Vera CPU, and Google's TPU v8t and v9, and

also Vera CPU probe cards, providing an additional growth engine beyond GPUs.
WinWay is well positioned in this segment due to its historical collaboration with end
customers and its proximity to the local Taiwanese test supply chain. We view AI CPUs
and TPUs as an increasingly important contributor to test interface demand as compute
architectures diversify in the agentic AI era, driving WinWay's socket and probe card
sales growth of 132%/75%/69% and 94%/72%/78% in 2026E/27E/28E, respectively.

We estimate CPU/TPU to contribute  around 30%/20% of WinWay's 2027E sales.
Valuation: Initiate with a Buy rating, with price target of NT$8,900
We forecast EPS of NT$101.8 in 2026E, rising to NT$201.4/393.0 in 2027E/28E. Our NT
$8,900 price target is based on 30x average 2027E-28E P/E, in line with the company's
historical average. We believe this valuation is supported by WinWay's exposure to
structural growth in AI/HPC testing, expanding socket content, and our long-term

earnings CAGR estimate of 30%. We initiate with a Buy rating.

Q: Will capacity constraints limit WinWay's growth in the next two to three years?
Near term, yes. Beyond 2026E, no. WinWay's current capacity remains constrained, but ongoing
expansions should significantly increase output over the next two years. With new capacity coming

online and the company shifting toward higher-value AI/HPC applications, including CPU probe
cards, >10k-pin coaxial sockets and next-generation HyperSockets, we forecast revenue growth of
105%/74%/71% in 2026E/27E/28E, supported by capacity additions, rising AI/HPC content and
expanding socket demand. We also expect operating leverage to improve as capacity scales and
utilization increases, with OPM rising from 27.5% in 2026E to 32.1%/36.7% in 2027E/28E.

Q: Will WinWay's GM recover from Q226's 38.3%?
Yes. We view recent margin pressure as largely temporary. GM has been impacted by MEMS probe
sourcing through Technoprobe and start-up costs associated with capacity expansion. Although
margin dilution from probe card business is unlikely to unravel due to the MEMS partnership terms
with Technoprobe, as new facilities ramp and yields improve, we expect profitability to recover
gradually from the Q226 trough, with gross margin improving to 43.1% in 2027E and 44.7% in

2028E.
We initiate coverage of WinWay with a Buy rating and NT$8,900 price target. We believe the market
is overly focused on near-term margin pressure and underappreciating WinWay's long-term growth
outlook. Key drivers include 1) expanding content from AI GPUs, CPUs, and TPUs, 2) substantial
capacity expansion, and 3) growing adoption of system-level testing (SLT). Together, these drivers
should support revenue growth of 105%/74%/71% in 2026E/27E/28E.

TSMC and OSATs have been expanding their CoWoS capacities aggressively, from 40kwpm in 2024
to around 90 kwpm in 2025, and is tracking to expand CoWoS capacity toward 160kwpm  in 2026E.
TSMC also ramped up expanding N3/N2 capacity, with multiple product launches from customers'
pipelines—Nvidia's Rubin, AMD's MI400 series/Venice CPU and other cloud ASICs—translating into
foreseeable, strong testing demand as the majority are WinWay's customers. AI accelerator test times

have increased meaningfully for new platforms. We expect continued front-end and advanced
packaging investments to drive higher testing intensity and support long-term demand for advanced
sockets and test interfaces.
WinWay's share price has corrected 43% from its April 2026 peak and currently trades at 16x of our
2028E P/E vs historical average of 30x. Our reverse DCF analysis indicates that the current market
price implies only a 19% long-term sales CAGR vs our estimate of 25%, alongside long-term GM

level of 32% vs our estimate of 45%. We see scope for re-rating.

WinWay was founded in 2001 in Kaoshiung, Taiwan. It is a semiconductor testing interface solutions
provider, with final test socket as  its main business segment. The company has high-end solutions for
AI/HPC testing, providing final test/system-level test and burn-in test sockets and probe cards through
its own made probes and partnership with Technoprobe.

Testing TAM expanding with IC complexity: leaders likely to
outperform industry growth
Our central view is that testing content per AI accelerator is growing faster than
semiconductor unit volumes, creating a multi-year expansion in testing TAM. We believe
semiconductor testing is entering a period of structural growth as AI-driven chip
complexity increases testing intensity across wafer, package and system levels. The

industry's shift-left strategy, which moves more testing upstream in the semiconductor
manufacturing process, is resulting in longer and more frequent test insertions. As cloud
AI GPUs and ASICs become increasingly complex, additional testing is required to ensure
functionality, performance and yield, while reducing defective parts per million (DPPM).
More robust testing should improve deployment efficiency and lower the cost of
operating AI infrastructure.

Semiconductor testing generally comprises four stages: wafer/chip probing (CP), final
test (FT), system-level test (SLT), and burn-in. CP is conducted at the wafer-level before
packaging, while FT, SLT, and burn-in occur after packaging. The below Figure 1S e m i c o n d u c t o r  t e s t i n g  p r o c e s s :  f r o m  w a f e r  l e v e l  t o  p a c k a g e  l e v e l
illustrates the semiconductor manufacturing flow.
Figure 1: Semiconductor testing process: from wafer level to package level

Source: UBS
We estimate semiconductor testing historically accounts for ~1% of total
semiconductor revenue. Within testing, consumables such as probe cards, sockets and
interface boards represent ~10-15% of industry revenue. According to Yole, the probe
card, socket and interface board markets should grow from US$3.8bn, US$2.3bn and
US$1.7bn in 2026 to US$5.5bn, US$3.1bn and US$2.0bn by 2031, respectively.

WinWay is expanding capacity rapidly to capture this growing TAM. Industry test
interface suppliers are expanding capacity aggressively to meet rising AI and HPC
demand. As advanced semiconductors require higher-performance probe cards, sockets
and interface boards, capacity additions are becoming increasingly important to support

growth across both wafer-level and package-level testing.
We believe WinWay is well positioned to gain share within the high-end AI/HPC test
interface market. Taiwanese suppliers benefit from their proximity to leading fabless
customers and the growing preference for localised supply chains, particularly for
advanced testing applications where responsiveness and engineering support are
critical. Against a backdrop of tight industry supply, WinWay announced significant

capacity expansion plans over the next two to three years.
WinWay's capacity is expected by the company to increase from 3.5mn pins per month
at year-end 2025 to 6mn currently, reaching 9mn by year-end 2026 and 14mn by year-
end 2027 through expansions in Renwu, Kaohsiung. By 2027, approximately 60% of
pogo-pin requirements should be internally sourced, strengthening both supply security
and margins.
We estimate these capacity additions will support revenue growth of 105%/74%/71%

in 2026E/27E/28E.

with its expanding capacity, strong customer relationships and proximity to Taiwan's
semiconductor testing ecosystem, including ASE/SPIL, KYEC and major handler
suppliers, WinWay is well positioned to gain share in the AI/HPC socket market.

### Page 6

WinWay Technology 8 October 2026 ab 6
The AI era has materially increased the value of semiconductor testing. Compared with
traditional mobile and automotive chips, AI accelerators incorporate larger compute
dies, more advanced nodes, chiplet architectures, advanced packaging technologies,
multiple I/O dies, and increasing HBM content. These factors drive higher-value test
consumables, including advanced MEMS probe cards, multilayer interface boards, and

next-generation sockets designed to support higher pin counts, thermal loading, and
power delivery requirements.
Capacity expansion by test interface vendors is ultimately supported by continued
investment throughout the advanced packaging ecosystem, from providers such as
TSMC, ASE, Intel, and Amkor. During the Q226 earnings calls, TSMC raised its recent
2026 capex from US$52-56bn to US$60-64bn and ASE raised its 2026 capex from US
$8.5bn to US$10.5bn, with both companies citing robust demand supporting capex

raise, we believe capex for the next two years will be even higher than 2026, indicating
continuous growth in future years. With advanced packaging technologies getting
significantly more complex and with larger form factor, illustrated by the upcoming
ramp of both TSMC's CoPoS (chip-on-panel-on-substrate) and Intel's EMIB-T
(embedded multi-die interconnect bridge) packaging technologies, test interface
solution providers will benefit with more value added consumables (see our CoPoS vs

EMIB-T deep dive).

New iterations of semiconductors, especially for AI / HPC applications, are set to increase
in both front-end wafer fabrication advancements and back-end packaging
complexities with each iterations (i.e. Nvidia's roadmap from Blackwell to Rubin to

Feynman or Google's TPU roadmap from v7 to v8 to v9).   This trend is evident with
TSMC's front-end  and TSMC, Intel, and OSAT's back-end advanced packaging capacity
expansions. For front-end, we see industry N3 capacity expanding from 156kwpm in
year end 2025 to 224/283/293kpwm in year end 2026E/27E/28E, and N2 capacity from
47kwpm in year-end 2025 to 111/200/312kpwm in year end 2026E/27E/28E. For back-
end, we are seeing industry CoWoS capacity to rise from 90kwpm in 2025 to 160kwpm

in 2026E and further expand to 270kwpm in 2027E.

Final-test, system-level test, and burn-in content should continue to increase as AI

accelerators become larger, more power-hungry and more complex. Rising thermal
design power and packaging complexity increase validation requirements, driving

### Page 7

WinWay Technology 8 October 2026 ab 7
longer test times and higher demand for test equipment to prevent testing from
becoming a production bottleneck. This is where WinWay's sockets comes into play—
as high-end chips with 2.5D/3D/3.5D packaging requires intricate FT sockets to handle
the high thermal design power (TDP) and high data rates of up to 224G during the FT
process for reliable test results.
Figure 10: Our FT test time estimates

Source: UBS estimates
While architectural changes can affect test requirements for individual product
generations, we expect the long-term trend of rising testing content to remain intact.
For example, Nvidia's Rubin Ultra adopts a simpler package structure than previously
expected, from a 4-die structure to a 2-die structure and Rubin Ultra HBM de-spec from
HBM4E 12-Hi to initially use HBM4 8-Hi and upgrade to HBM4E 8-Hi at the latter stages,

which may moderate near-term test growth. However, future products such as Nvidia's
Feynman are expected to reintroduce greater package complexity, with a four-GPU per
package structure. More importantly, any reduction in test time can be offset by higher
production volumes, making throughput requirements equally important for testing
demand.
We expect test interface content growth to outpace unit growth, For instance, test
socket's ASP directly correlates with its pin counts, with the addition of 20-30% more

pins inside the sockets, socket ASP can grow 20-30% or more as the socket might
require significant cooling capabilities as the TDP of the DUT rises with its larger form
factor. Unlike prior semiconductor cycles, much of the current demand is driven by rising
test interface content per device rather than unit growth alone.
Robust growth drivers across semiconductor testing
We believe WinWay is set to benefit from several semiconductor testing growth drivers

and capture the AI induced, expanding testing TAM. We see three main growth drivers
for WinWay: 1) AI accelerators (GPU + TPU), 2) agentic AI driven CPU demand, and 3)
shift-left testing trend. Below we list the three main growth drivers in detail.
Accelerators still in focus with TPU as another significant growth driver
Nvidia has been the primary driver of testing content growth over the past several years,
benefiting the entire semiconductor testing supply chain. We expect this trend to

continue as GPU architectures evolve from Blackwell to Rubin and then to Feynman,
with increasing process node complexity, larger package sizes and more advanced
packaging technologies driving higher testing value per device.
We believe Nvidia should remain a major contributor to  WinWay, with GPUs continuing
to serve as their core growth engine. We estimate WinWay's socket share could improve
from a roughly 50/50 split with Smiths Interconnect in prior generations to 60/40 with

Rubin. Nvidia currently accounts for approximately 50% of WinWay's revenue. Both
Nvidia GPUs and Google TPUs increase testing demand through higher content per
device rather than unit growth alone.

### Page 8

WinWay Technology 8 October 2026 ab 8
Beyond Nvidia, Google's TPU roadmap represents a significant incremental growth
opportunity.  In our view, WinWay should benefit as the socket supplier for both TPU v8t
and the next-generation TPU v9 platform. We believe MediaTek's increasing share gain
for Google TPUs will also benefit the local Taiwanese test interface supply chain. We
estimate TPU to contribute to around 20% of WinWay 2027E sales.
We view TPU v9 as a particularly attractive opportunity given its large four-die package

architecture based on Intel's EMIB-T packaging technology, which should drive higher
testing content. We estimate production of 4mn TPU v8t units in 2027E, followed by
2.3mn TPU v9 packages (9.2mn dies) and an additional 2mn TPU v8t units in 2028E.
Importantly, while EMIB-T packaging is expected to be performed in Malaysia, we
believe the majority of advanced testing value should remain within the Taiwanese
testing ecosystem. Leading testing providers, including ASE and KYEC, maintain

substantial testing operations across Taiwan and Southeast Asia, supporting continued
participation by Taiwanese test interface suppliers. See our latest MediaTek report and
EMIB-T deep dive.
CPU business as a supplemental catalyst for test interface companies in
the agentic world
We believe semiconductor test interface are an underappreciated beneficiary of the
emerging agentic AI ecosystem. UBS estimates the CPU market will expand to US
$170bn by 2030E from US$31bn in 2025 and US$71bn in 2027E, driven by rising

deployment of AI infrastructure. As CPU-to-GPU ratios increasingly approach 1:1 in AI
clusters, demand for testing interfaces should expand alongside CPU volumes. TSMC is
the main CP test provider in the industry, with probe card suppliers aiding its massive
demand by supplying probes, such as TSMC's GPU probe cards supported by
Technoprobe's probes. However, with capacity now becoming scarce with strong front
and back-end demand, TSMC is outsourcing its CP business to ASE and KYEC, further

broadening the testing supply chain. This is where new probe card vendors come in—
smaller probe card players in the past, such as  WinWay, now has the opportunity to gain
significant, non-GPU share from TSMC, as they have MEMS probe capabilities
(WinWay's MEMS probe are from its strategic partnership with Technoprobe) and has a
long established relationship with the fabless companies such as Nvidia via supplying
non-probe card consumables such as sockets. Of which, CPU projects has become the

main area for  WinWay to expand their footprint.
We expect WinWay to benefit from the ramp of Nvidia's ARM-based Vera CPU and
Google's ARM-based Axion CPU in 2026E-27E. WinWay is positioned to supply sockets
and probe cards for Vera and sockets for Axion. Additional upside could come from
AMD's Venice CPU, where WinWay is well positioned. For WinWay, CPU-related
demand could account for approximately 30% of sales in 2026E-27E. Importantly, CPU
programmes diversify exposure beyond AI GPUs and provide a second structural growth

driver for test interface suppliers.
Shift-left igniting secular growth for chip probing
The industry's adoption of shift-left testing should drive a multi-year structural growth
opportunity for chip probing (CP) and its supply chain. As advanced packaging costs rise
and AI accelerators become increasingly complex, semiconductor manufacturers are
placing greater emphasis on identifying known-good dies (KGD) before packaging. The
test shift-left trend will  drive structural AI/HPC probe card and test consumables

demand, with increasing test interface content as test insertions and test time increase.
WinWay should also benefit through its partnership with Technoprobe, with probe card
revenue growth remaining robust at 94%/72% in 2026E/27E following 161%/260%
growth in 2024/25.

The economics of advanced packaging reinforce this trend. Heterogeneous integration,
chiplet architectures and 2.5D/3D packaging significantly increase package value, while

defects identified late in the manufacturing process can lead to substantial scrap costs.
To mitigate this risk, foundries and OSATs such as TSMC and ASE employ distributed
testing flows incorporating KGD, known-good-stack (KGS) and assembly test screening,
to catch defects before the components enter a multi-week packaging cycle (>2 weeks
for CoW and 2 weeks for oS). The importance of these tests rises as AI clusters become
increasingly dependent on component reliability and low defective-parts-per-million

(DPPM) rates. Given the multiplicative yield degradation, a 300bps yield decline at just
one manufacturing stage (e.g. dropping from 99.5% to 96.5%) reduces overall
integrated four-stage yield from 98.0% to 95.0%; on a 500,000 unit ramp at US
$10,000 per unit, this 3% scrap delta translates into around US$150m in unrecoverable
losses given >6-month manufacturing lead times.
At the same time, rising chip power consumption is increasing the technical difficulty of

wafer-level testing. As chip thermal design power scales beyond 1,000W and power
density is expected by the industry to surge  another 8x by 2030, traditional wafer-level
chip probe (WLCP) encounters severe physical bottlenecks. Finer probe pitches and
higher power densities increase the risk of overheating, probe needle burnout and yield
degradation, creating demand for more advanced probing and thermal-control
solutions.

WinWay set to benefit from the AI trend with its leading

WinWay  was founded in 2001 in Kaohsiung, Taiwan, and is a leading provider of
semiconductor testing interface solutions, with test sockets representing its core
business. The company focuses on advanced AI/HPC applications, supplying sockets for

final test (FT), system-level test (SLT) and burn-in, as well as cobra probe cards  and
partnership with Technoprobe for MEMS probe cards.
WinWay's business is primarily divided into two segments:
 Test socket: Sockets are WinWay's largest revenue contributor. The company
supplies a broad range of testing sockets, including coaxial, PoP/plastic and burn-
in sockets, serving applications ranging from consumer electronics to high-
performance computing.

 Coaxial socket: utilised as the mainstream option for nearly all AI/HPC chips,
as traditional sockets are near its physical limits. Coaxial sockets need to handle
data rates of up to 224G for high speed transmission and high chip TDP during
the FT process.
 HyperSocket: a rubber-silicone like layer called elastomer are placed on top
of the pogo pins, these elastomer pins causes zero damage to the chips being
tested under high contact forces. WinWay's next generation HyperSocket

technology utilises elastomers to provide a softer contact between the DUT
and the pogo pins, avoiding damage to solder ball. This structure significantly
enhances spring probe's lifetime, reduces cleaning frequency, and achieves
maximum production efficiency. We estimate it will contribute around 5% of
sales in 2027E with a 20-30% ASP premium as compared to standard coaxial
socket.
 Probe cards:  WinWay historically supplies cobra probe cards (vertical probe

cards), but with the company announcing their five-year probe supply deal with
Technoprobe for MEMS probe in December 2025, they are now one of Nvida Vera
CPU's main probe card suppliers.
AI accelerators are pushing socket requirements beyond 10,000 pins, making coaxial
sockets the industry standard for leading-edge AI/HPC devices. These sockets must
support transmission speeds of up to 224G while handling significantly higher thermal
design power (TDP) during testing.

Elastomer technology is increasingly being adopted alongside spring-probe (pogo-pin)
architectures to reduce chip damage and improve contact reliability. WinWay's next-
generation HyperSocket integrates elastomer materials between the DUT and spring
probes, improving probe lifetime, reducing cleaning requirements and enhancing
production efficiency. We believe HyperSocket could become an important
differentiator as next-generation AI accelerators demand higher pin counts, bandwidth

and thermal performance. We estimate HyperSocket could contribute approximately
5% of WinWay's revenue in 2027E while carrying a 20-30% ASP premium versus
conventional coaxial sockets. The transition toward larger AI packages, higher pin
counts and faster data-transfer speeds should continue to increase socket content per
device. We expect this trend to support long-term growth in the socket market, with
technology leaders such as WinWay well positioned to gain share through next-

generation solutions including HyperSocket.
Beyond its existing product portfolio, WinWay is developing next-generation
technologies including CPO electrical test sockets and liquid-cooled sockets for future
high TDP AI accelerators, reinforcing its position within the AI/HPC testing ecosystem.

WinWay's competitive advantages are its 1) technology leadership with coaxial socket
and HyperSocket, 2) proximity to the semiconductor testing value chain in Taiwan, and
3) historical relationship with key AI companies such as Nvidia and AMD. We believe

these advantages may allow WinWay to capture significant upside in the growing
semiconductor testing TAM driven by cloud AI proliferation, and gain share across its
competition such as Smiths Interconnect for AI and HPC applications.

Compared with the probe card market, the socket industry is more fragmented.
Competition include:
 WinWay (6515.TW) : A major share gainer in AI/HPC sockets, with strong
exposure to Nvidia and AMD. The company specializes in high-performance
coaxial sockets and is developing its next-generation HyperSocket platform. It is

also expanding into probe cards through its partnership with Technoprobe.

and memory sockets.

exposure.

### Page 13

WinWay Technology 8 October 2026 ab 13
WinWay's margin is set to recover in 2027E/28E
Company GM hit a trough in Q226, at 38.3%. We view recent margin pressure as
largely temporary. GM has been impacted by MEMS probe sourcing through
Technoprobe (probe card GM now below 40% and dilutive to >50% coaxial socket GM)
and start-up costs associated with WinWay's rapid capacity expansion. During the early
part of 2025, WinWay's GM was high at 48.9% in H125, with more cobra probe cards

(60% GM) in mix with solid pogo pin and socket yield. With MEMS probe card now
accounting for more than 70% of probe card revenue and GM at around low to mid
30%, margin dilution from probe card business is unlikely to unravel for GM to return to
above 45% in the near term due to the MEMS partnership terms with Technoprobe.
However, we expect that as new facilities ramp and yields improve, profitability to
recover gradually from the Q226 trough. We see GM improving to 43.1% in 2027E and

44.7% in 2028E.

Appendix: What are semiconductor test sockets?
Semiconductor test socket are electro-mechanical interface that are used post chip
probing and chip packaging process to test the functionality of the chips. Sockets are
placed onto load boards on top of testers during the final test/system level test
processes. Handlers will place the chips onto the sockets, and pins inside the sockets will
enable the testers to read the signals from the device under test (DUT, i.e. the packaged

chips), with tester signals fan out  from beneath the PCB load board.
Figure 27: Socket illustration (with load
board underneath)
Source: UBS
As AI accelerators become larger and more complex, socket requirements continue to
increase. High-end sockets typically comprise a socket housing, alignment plate,
integrated thermal-control components and contact elements that connect the DUT to
the tester. Spring probes (pogo pins) are the most common choice for HPC sockets, with

high durability, individual probe replacement flexibility, and consistent contact force. FT
and SLT sockets account for more than 70% of the total socket market, with burn-in
sockets representing the remainder.

Despite a 120% YTD share-price increase, WinWay remains 43% below its April 2026
peak and trades at only 16x our 2028E P/E. We believe the market remains overly
focused on near-term GM pressure while underestimating the company's multi-year
growth opportunity. Our reverse DCF analysis indicates that the current market price
implies only a 19% long-term sales CAGR vs our estimate of 25%, alongside long-term
GM level of 32% vs our estimate of 45%.

We believe investors are not fully reflecting three key drivers:
1) CPU and TPU opportunities are becoming increasingly meaningful.
Beyond its core GPU socket franchise, WinWay is positioned to benefit from Nvidia's
Vera CPU, AMD's Venice CPU, and Google's TPU. These programmes should broaden
revenue sources and create an additional demand driver for high-end sockets and probe
cards.
2) Larger AI packages are driving higher socket content per device.

As AI accelerators adopt larger form factors, higher pin counts and greater power
consumption, socket complexity and ASPs continue to rise. We expect socket content
growth to outpace semiconductor unit growth, creating a structural tailwind for
WinWay.
3) Capacity ramp and margin recovery are not fully reflected.
Following substantial capacity expansion, we expect revenue growth of
105%/74%/71% in 2026E/27E/28E. At the same time, we forecast gross margin

recovery from 38.3% in Q226 to 43.1%/44.7% in 2027E/28E as utilization and yields
improve.
Our NT$8,900 price target is based on our valuation for WinWay at 30x average 2027E-
28E P/E, in-line with its historical average of 30x and underpinned by a 30% 2027-31E
earnings CAGR. Comparing with historical NTM peer average PE of roughly 25x
(currently trading at 39x NTM PE), we believe WinWay deserves to trade at a premium
given its leadership in AI/HPC sockets, expanding CPU and TPU exposure, and our long-

term earnings CAGR estimate.
WinWay is trading at NT$6,130 as of
6 Oct 2026

Company Description
Company Description
WinWay was founded in 2001 in Kaoshiung, Taiwan. It is a
semiconductor testing interface solution provider, with final test
sockets as its main business segment. The company has high-end
solutions for AI/HPC testing, providing final test/system-level test
and burn-in test sockets and probe cards through its own-made
probes and a partnership with Technoprobe.

Industry Outlook
New iterations of semiconductors, especially for AI / HPC
applications, are set to increase in both front-end wafer
fabrication advancements and back-end packaging complexities
with each iterations. With the industry now moves to utilize
"shift left", chip probing and its relevant supply chain will be key
beneficiaries of this trend which we believe will unlock multi-
year, secular growth trends.

Forecast excess return 39.9%
Company Description
WinWay was founded in 2001 in Kaoshiung, Taiwan. It is a semiconductor testing interface
solution provider, with final test sockets as its main business segment. The company has high-
end solutions for AI/HPC testing, providing final test/system-level test and burn-in test sockets

and probe cards through its own-made probes and a partnership with Technoprobe.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value WinWay based on PE methodology.
Risks for the semis industry:
- Geopolitical uncertainties: Geopolitical uncertainties including tariffs and more export
control could negatively impact semis cycle, and a downturn would lead to slower demand in
the overall market and moderating capacity expansion.
- Meaningful deceleration of new technology development: Slower demand and less

investment are expected If there is meaningful deceleration of new technology development
such as Cloud AI, autonomous driving, etc.
- Rising utility cost: Larger-than-expected utility cost increases would further pressure
profitability. Rising utility costs in Taiwan have negatively impacted margins for Taiwan tech
companies.
Company risks:
- Faster- or slower-than-expected CoWoS capacity expansion: This could result in WinWay
gaining/losing socket/probe card orders from major end customers, foundries, and OSATs.

- Competitors achieving technological advancement: Potential risk of competition (from
either local or foreign companies) achieving technological advancements could result in
WinWay losing market share from major end customers, foundries and OSATs.
- Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth: Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth could
lead to a ramp-up or a slowdown of equipment orders from major foundries and OSATs to
WinWay, affecting long-term revenue growth potential.

- Slowdown or delay of new technological advancement: A potential slowdown or delay of
the rollout for technological advancements for technologies such as SoIC, CoPoS, and CPO,
would limit future socket/probe card orders.
- Increase of raw material costs: Rising raw material costs may directly impact WinWay’s
margins for socket/probe card shipments.

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 1

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關
條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保證。非經本公司同意，不得
將本報告加以引用或轉載。 
1 
6667 信紘科 (TRUSVAL)

報告日期： 2026/10/05            研究員：王蕎程 (Joe Wang) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要: 
 訂單滿載與高能見度：信紘科現有訂單達 230 億元，受惠於
AI 與半導體擴建潮，廠務設備與二次配工程訂單滿載，能見度
看至 2028 年。  
 大型統包與海外擴張：業務從單一工程升級至數十億級大型機
電統包，並結合策略夥伴進軍美、日、德、新等海外市場，帶
動營收成長。  
 綠色永續與持續收益：成立專責子公司銳源環境科技，聚焦廢
液處理與資源循環，將一次性工程建置延伸至維修與代操等持
續性收入。

一. 高科技建廠需求強勁，1H26 稅後 EPS 達 7.70 元 
信紘科以特氣特化廠務供應設備系統與二次配裝機工程為主要業
務，近年營運重心與服務範疇持續升級至二次配統包以及機電統
包工程。2025 年高科技產業營收達 59.45 億元，占整體營收高達 
93.56%；2026 年第二季高科技產業營收仍占絕大多數，半導體與
高科技建廠已成為公司最核心且穩定的營收來源

2026 年上半年營收達 32.19 億元；營業毛利 7.49 億元， 毛利率 
23.25% ；營業利益 3.55 億元，稅後淨利 3.79 億元，累計 EPS 達 
7.70 元。目前公司現有在手訂單高達 230 億元，訂 單能見度已直
達 2028 年，整體營運維持強勁成長動能。

二.半導體擴建為主要成長動能，工程與統包範疇擴大 
在廠務與工程服務部分，信紘科目前量產與工程實績包含特氣特
化廠務供應設備、系統建置及二次配管線裝機工程。為進一步提
升單案規模與營運效能，公司將服務領域延伸至二次配統包與大
型機電統包（GC/EPC），使專案金額邁入數十億級規模。後續業
務擴張重點，亦包含綠色製造與循環經濟，於 2025 年 12 月成
立專責子公司「銳源環境科技」，聚焦廢液處理與資源循環，將
一次性建置工程延伸至維修、營運代操及再生產品銷售等多元商
業模式等長期持續性收入。

目前主要服務據點皆配合客戶全球擴產需求建置，公司在台灣擁
有苗栗、台中、台南等據點，憑藉完整的本土供應鏈與人力調度，
能就近支援各大晶圓廠新建、擴建與改建之即時需求（主系統工

三. 預估 2026 / 2027 年稅後 EPS 14.76 / 16.83 元 
預估 2026 全年營收 71.17 億元，YoY +12.0%；營業毛利 16.41 
億元，YoY +27.1%，毛利率 23.06%；營業利益 7.92 億元，稅後
純益 7.57 億元，稅後 EPS 14.76 元。展望 2027 年，受惠全球
半導體廠與先進封裝建廠需求熱、在手 230 億元訂單高能見度，
加上大型 GC 統包工程持續認列與海外業務擴張，預計 2027 年
營收 78.29 億元，YoY +10.0%，營業毛利 18.18 億元，毛利率 
23.22% ，營業利益 9.76 億元，稅後純益 8.29 億元，稅後 EPS 為 
16.83 元。

四. 投資建議為「買進」，目標價 330 元 
綜合考量 (1)先進製程相關半導體需求維持強勁 (2) 公司可參與
的工程範圍擴大至海外及機電統包等大型工程，並觀察歷史及同
業本益比區間 ， 目前本益比 16 倍評價偏低 ， 故投資建議為「買進」，
目標價 330 元（20x 2027EPS）。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
信紘科技（Trusval Technology，股票代號：6667）成立於 1995 年，
深耕高科技製造供應鏈布局，並由既有廠務供應設備系統服務，
逐步拓展至機電統包、建廠總承攬及整合型工程解決方案。目前
業務範疇涵蓋廠務設備、製程二次配裝機服務，以及高科技廠房
整合建置與機電統包工程，可依客戶建廠及擴產需求，提供從工
程規劃、設計、採購、施工、測試驗證至系統整合與專案管理的
一站式服務。憑藉跨工種整合、精密製程系統建置及大型專案管
理能力，公司持續提升高附加價值工程案件比重，協助客戶縮短
建廠時程、確保工程品質並提升營運效率。因應全球淨零轉型與
資源循環需求，信紘科技亦積極發展製程機能水設備、廢液處理
與循環經濟解方、資源零廢中心建置等業務，將工程整合能力延
伸至綠色製造與永續基礎建設。

台灣為公司最核心且成熟的基本盤，憑藉超過 30 年高科技產物
工程的深厚經驗，累積了卓越的技術能力與執行實力；得益於台
灣完整的高標準供應鏈以及敏捷的人力與材料調度，能高效支援
客戶新建、擴建及改建廠需求，加上工程需求穩定且執行週期較
短（主系統約 6 至 8 個月，二次配約 4 至 6 個月），構築了
公司長期穩健的營業基礎。

為打造未來成長新引擎，公司順應客戶海外建廠的全球布局與緊
湊時程，將成熟的工程能力輸出海外，信紘科不僅以既有產物工
程為切入點，逐步擴展至純水、廢水、無塵室及機電等領域以擴
大單一案件服務範疇，在非美日等新興區域更結合策略夥伴共同
承攬；同時，公司統包能力持續升級，從早期高技術門檻的二次
配工程，拓展至跨系統二次配統包，進而邁向承接大規模、高整
合度的跨國 GC 與工程管理角色，透過整合台外資源提升接單層
級與案件金額，全面貼近客戶全球建廠需求。

二.產業趨勢概況 
市場概況 
A.半導體產業概況  
半導體產業為全球數位經濟之核心基礎建設，近年在人工智慧
（AI）、高效能運算（HPC）及邊緣運算應用加速普及帶動下，產
業需求結構出現顯著變化。先進製程與高密度運算晶片需求持續
提升，使晶圓廠投資動能自庫存調整週期中快速復甦，並進入新
一輪資本支出擴張階段。 根據國際半導體產業協會（SEMI）2025
年發布之預測，全球 300mm 晶圓廠設備支出於 2026 年至 2028 年
間累計將達約 3,740 億美元，其中 2025 年單年度支出將首次突破
1,000 億美元水準。顯示全球半導體建廠投資已由短期循環性波
動，逐步轉為結構性成長態勢。另依 Deloitte（2025）預估，全球
半導體銷售額有機會於 2030 年前後突破 1 兆美元規模，反映產業
長期需求動能仍具成長潛力。

B.半導體產業 ESG 趨勢 
隨淨零排放與國際永續規範逐步落實，ESG 已由合規要求轉為半
導體供應鏈的重要競爭要素。依 SNS Insider（ 2025）預估，全球
半導體廢液處理市場將由 2024 年的 27.2 億美元成長至 2032 年的
49.2 億美元，其中亞太地區受惠於晶圓廠持續擴建與永續轉型，
成長動能可望高於全球平均。在此趨勢下，半導體廠務環保技術
逐步朝 「資源再生、能源轉化、源頭減量」三大方向發展。首先，
硫酸、氨氮廢水及有機溶劑等製程廢液，正由傳統末端處理轉向
回收再生，經純化後重新作為工業級或電子級原料使用，提高資
源循環率並降低廢棄物處置需求。其次，有機廢液處理逐步導入
Waste-to-Energy 技術，將廢液轉化為燃料或能源使用， 以降低外購
能源需求與碳排放。

此外，先進製程亦更加重視源頭減量，透過綠色化學品替代與先
進流體製程，降低強酸、強鹼及水資源使用量。隨 3nm 以下製程
持續發展，兼具降低化學品耗用、廢水負荷與營運成本效益的技
術，導入需求可望提升。 
整體而言，未來半導體廠務市場將由單純設備供應，逐步轉向整

## Document 105: 9月美国CPI数据点评：通胀超预期之下，加息预期剧烈波动

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月美国CPI数据点评：通胀超预期之下，加息预期剧烈波动.pdf
Report source: 交银国际证券
Date: 2026-10-17
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

此报告最后部分的分析师披露、商业关系披露和免责声明为报告的一部分，必须阅读。
下载本公司之研究报告，可从彭博搜寻NH BCM  或 登录研究部网站https://research.bocomgroup.com 
交银国际研究 
宏观策略

2026年10月16日
全球宏观 
通胀超预期之下，加息预期剧烈波动——9月美国CPI数据点评
美国通胀回升，核心通胀超预期，核心价格压力有所扩散。9月美国CPI环比
上涨0.6%，较8月的0.4%进一步加速，同比由3.4%升至3.7%，高于市场预期
的3.6%；核心CPI环比上涨0.3%，与8月持平，高于预期的0.2%，同比由8
月的2.4%升至2.6%。总体通胀回升主要由能源推动，但核心通胀环比上涨显
示，通胀回落进程面临新的阻力。
食品通胀整体温和，能源继续成为推升总通胀的主要力量。 食品价格环比持
平、同比上涨2.5%，其中家庭食品下降0.1%，外出就餐上涨0.2%。能源价格
环比上涨5.2%、同比上涨20.4%，其中汽油环比大涨9.1%、同比上涨34.2%，
贡献当月CPI涨幅的一半以上；燃油价格环比上涨15.3%。中东地缘冲突导致
能源供应持续受扰，汽柴油价格高企不仅直接推升通胀，还可能通过运输及
生产成本向其他商品价格传导。
核心商品通胀有所加速，核心服务通胀粘性增强。核心商品环比上涨0.3%，
高于8月的0.1%，同比上涨0.7%。其中二手车价格上涨0.7%，新车价格上涨
0.2%，服装上涨0.3%，医疗护理商品则下降0.7%。核心服务环比由0.3%升至
0.4%、同比上涨3.1%。其中住房环比上涨0.4%，业主等价租金上涨0.3%，住
宿上涨2.0%；交通服务上涨0.6%，航空票价上涨1.2%、同比涨22.4%；汽车
维修、医院服务分别上涨0.7%，教育上涨0.6%。住房仍是核心通胀的重要支
撑，而交通、教育及部分医疗服务价格上涨，显示剔除住房后的服务通胀压
力也不容忽视。
展望未来，能源冲击与服务价格粘性或使通胀在年底前维持高位。10月汽柴
油价格仍是最大不确定性，其向运输、食品和商品价格的传导可能尚未结束。
到年底，若油价维持高位，总体CPI同比或持续高于3%，核心通胀回落也将
较为缓慢。进入2027年，若中东能源供应改善、住房租金滞后回落及工资增
速放缓，通胀有望重新进入下降通道；反之，能源与关税冲击的持续传导可
能使通胀高位运行更长时间。
美联储政策方面，通胀超预期推动美联储进一步加息的概率剧烈波动。 数据
公布前，市场对10月加息25基点的定价约为两成，公布后一度升至45%—
50%，随后回落到约两成多的水平。我们认为，考虑到能源供给冲击无法通
过加息直接解决，叠加就业增长有所放缓，美联储仍可能选择10月暂时观
望，但通胀二轮传导风险有所上升，12月加息仍是基准情形。
市场反应：呈现股市下跌、美元走强的特征。 CPI公布当日收盘，道指、标普
500和纳指全线下跌，分别下跌约0.09%、1.11%和1.88%，科技股特别是半导
体承压明显，金融股受银行财报影响表现分化，能源相关资产则受到高油价
支撑。2年期美债收益率冲高回落到收盘上行约3基点，10年期美债收益率盘
中上升，但收盘下降2个基点；美元指数上涨约0.04%；黄金下跌约1.6%；布
伦特原油维持在每桶106美元附近。整体来看，市场重新定价美联储加息路
径，利率敏感型资产面临压力。


王博群, PhD, FRM
hugh.wang@bocomgroup.com

### Page 6

评级定义
分析员个股评级定义 : 分析员行业评级定义：
买入：预期个股未来12个月的总回报高于相关行业。
中性：预期个股未来12个月的总回报与相关行业一致。
沽出：预期个股未来12个月的总回报低于相关行业
无评级：对于个股未来12个月的总回报与相关行业的比较，
分析员并无确信观点。
领先：分析员预期所覆盖行业未来12个月的表现相对于大盘
标杆指数具吸引力。
同步：分析员预期所覆盖行业未来12个月的表现与大盘标杆
指数一致。
落后：分析员预期所覆盖行业未来12个月的表现相对于大盘
标杆指数不具吸引力。
香港市场的标杆指数为恒生综合指数，A股市场的标杆指数
为MSCI中国A股指数，美国上市中概股的标杆指数为标普美
国中概股50（美元）指数

### Page 7

公司的高级人员（包括就房地产基金而言，担任该房地产基金的管理公司的高级人员；及就任何其他实体而言，在该实体中担任负责管理该
等公司的高级人员或其同级人员）；iii）他们及他们之相关有联系者并没拥有于本报告内涉及其评论的任何公司的证券之任何财务利益。
根据证监会持牌人或注册人操守准则第16.2段，“有联系者”指：i）分析员的配偶、亲生或领养的未成年子女，或未成年继子女；ii）某
信托的受托人，而分析员、其配偶、其亲生或领养的未成年子女或其未成年继子女是该信托的受益人或酌情对象；或iii）惯于或有义务按
照分析员的指示或指令行事的另一人。

事件 
2026年9月，CPI同比增速0.9%（市场预期1%），前值0.8%， 环比增速0.2%， 前值0.4%，
1-9月CPI累计同比0.9%；PPI同比增速5%（市场预期4.4%） ， 较上月提高1.2个百分点，
环比增速1.2%，前值0.4%，1-9月PPI累计同比2.3%。 
1    非食品项改善支撑 CPI 增速，能源相关分项较强 
CPI同比增速延续回升，非食品项持续改善，为主要支撑项。9月，CPI同比增速由上
月的0.8%回升至0.9%，略低于市场预期，非食品项为主要支撑因素。其中，CPI食品
项同比增速由上月的-1.4%回落至-1.7%，环比持平于0.4%。非食品项同比由1.2%升至
1.5%，环比增速由上月的0.3%小幅下行至0.2%。 
图表1：CPI同比增速延续回升（%） 图表2：CPI食品项环比处于历史同期较弱水平（%）

资料来源：iFind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：iFind，万联证券研究所 
核心CPI、 服务项和消费品同比增速持平， 环比增速下滑。核心CPI同比增速持平于1%，
环比0%， 前值0.1%。CPI服务项同比持平于0.8%， 环比-0.2%， 前值0.1%； 消费品同比
增速则由0.8%升至1%，环比0.5%，前值0.6%。

证券研究报告|宏观报告

万联证券研究所 www.wlzq.cn 第 4 页 共 8 页  
猪肉价格转为下跌，鲜菜价格弱于季节性，鲜果延续季节性回落。9月猪肉价格环比
转跌，部分原因是集团猪企为规避节后风险在节前集中出栏， 散户跟随行动， 另一方
面， 双节备货力度不及预期， 导致供应增速高于需求增速，出现“旺季不旺” 的现象。
预计后续随着产能去化效果显现， 猪肉价格将逐步企稳。牛羊肉价格延续上涨。鲜菜
价格下跌，弱于季节性；鲜果价格则延续季节性回落。 
从CPI食品项具体分项来看，9月，猪肉价格同比增速继续回升1.8个百分点至-10%，
下跌速度减缓， 环比增速由上月的1.3%小幅回落至1.2%；牛、羊肉同比增速则分别由
上月的5.3%、6.3%上行至7.5%、6.4%。水产品同比由-0.6%升至0.4%，蛋类同比增速
由15%回落至14.3%，环比2%。鲜菜价格同比增速由-2.8%降至-8.1%，环比降至0.5%；
鲜果价格同比由-0.5%回落至-1.5%，环比由负转正， 升至0.6%。粮食、 食用油、 烟草、
酒类同比增速分别由8月的-0.6%、-1.3%、-0.7%、-2.9%变动为-0.6%、-1.1%、-0.8%、
-3.1%。

燃料同比由8.3%继续升至14.9%，环比4.5%，受国际油价高企带动；水电燃料同比增

资料来源：iFind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：iFind，万联证券研究所 
2    国际油价带动 PPI 走高，AI 产业维持高增速 
PPI同比升至2022年以来新高，生产资料为主要拉动，耐用消费品增幅扩大。国际原
油价格高企，输入性通胀压力明显，PPI同、环比均上行，其中同比增速录得2022年
以来新高。PPIRM同比增速上升，幅度与PPI接近。PPI生产资料为主要拉动项，其中
原料工业、 采掘工业、 加工工业涨幅均扩大， 加工工业与原材料工业剪刀差走阔。生
活资料同比跌幅继续收窄， 其中衣着跌幅持平，食品、一般日用品收窄，耐用消费品
增速延续上行。 
具体数据来看，PPI同比5%，较上月上行1.2%个百分点，高于市场预期的4.4%，环比
增速1.2%，前值0.4%。工业生产者购进价格指数（PPIRM）同比由5.8%上行至6.9%，
环比1.2%， 前值0.3%。PPI生产资料同比增速上升1.5个百分点至6.5%， 其中， 采掘工
业、 原料工业和加工工业同比增速分别由上月的17.8%、6.7%、3.1%变动至25.4%、9.4%、
3.4%。PPI生活资料同比跌幅收窄0.4个百分点至-0.1%，环比增速0.1%，前值0.2%；
其中， 衣着同比跌幅维持在-1.2%， 食品同比跌幅收窄至-1.8%，一般日用品跌幅缩小
至-0.6%，耐用消费品同比由1.2%升至1.6%。

国际油价高企带动能化产业链通胀升温，黑色金属产业价格表现较弱。采掘业分项同
比增速分化，环比普遍上行，其中石油和天然气开采业同比增速大幅上行，从10.5%
上行至24.5%， 环比增速10.4%。 黑色金属矿采选业同比增速下行， 由1.1%回落至-3.4%，
环比跌幅小幅收窄； 有色金属矿采选业同比增速小幅上行0.3个百分点至21.4%， 环比
由负转正。 煤炭开采和洗选业同比由26.6%上行至35.3%， 环比9.5%。 中游加工制造业
通胀升温， 石油、 煤炭及其他燃料加工业同比从11.1%升至21.6%， 化学原料和化学制
品制造业同比增速上行3个百分点至12.1%，化学纤维制造业同比上行2.7个百分点至
12.3%。下游加工业中有色金属冶炼表现较强，黑色金属冶炼和压延加工业同比由 -
0.3%微升至-0.2%，有色金属冶炼和压延加工业同比增速上行0.6个百分点至21.4%。
非金属矿物制品业同比跌幅收窄0.7个百分点至-2.5%，环比0.3%。 
下游高端制造业价格走势分化， 汽车跌幅收窄，AI产业维持强势。下游汽车制造业同
比跌幅收窄0.2个百分点至-2%，环比-0.2%。铁路、船舶等运输设备制造业同比持平
于0.1%，环比0。计算机、通信和其他电子设备制造业维持强势，同比由5.3%进一步
升至5.8%， 环比0.4%， 前值0.6%。AI、 算电协同加快发展带动相关行业需求增加， 计
算机通信和其他电子设备制造业、电气机械和器材制造业合计拉动 PPI环比增速约
0.07个百分点。电力、 热力生产和供应业同比跌幅扩大、 燃气生产和供应业增速上行，
水的生产和供应业增速持平。

3    总结与展望 
输入性通胀带动生产端普涨，消费端刚性持续。9月国际油价高企，能化产业链通胀
持续升温，生活资料亦上行，PPI同比增速升至2022年以来新高。核心CPI环比持平、
民生服务价格刚性持续， 显示价格内生动能仍有待改善。随着四季度稳增长政策出台
并有序落地，预期内需逐步企稳改善。AI产业价格表现延续强势，持续为PPI带来向
上动力。

## Document 149: AIと金融市場、強まる相互依存 ～IMFの国際金融安定性報告書から読み解く新たなリスク～

Source PDF: all report pdf/AIと金融市場、強まる相互依存 ～IMFの国際金融安定性報告書から読み解く新たなリスク～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Flash Insights

2026 年10 月 15 日

1/5 
AI と金融市場、強まる相互依存 
～IMF の国際金融安定性報告書から読み解く新たなリスク～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  奥田 宏二

1. はじめに 
国際通貨基金（IMF）は10 月 13 日、国際金融安定性報告書（Global Financial Stability 
Report、GFSR）の全体版を公表した。金融市場は底堅さを維持していると評価したうえで、金融
安定上のリスクは高いとした。主なリスクとして①人工知能（AI）関連資産の大幅な値下がり、
②国債利回りの急上昇、③サイバー攻撃の三つを取り上げた。 
GFSRは世界経済の動向を分析する「世界経済見通し」 （WEO）と並ぶIMFの主要な定期報告書だ。
株式・債券市場や銀行、ノンバンクなどを幅広く点検し、金融システムの安定を損なうリスクや
ショックを増幅させる脆弱性を分析する。各国の金融当局や市場関係者にとって、その時々の金
融市場・システムの課題を示すものとして注目度が高い。本稿では、GFSRが今回示した三つのリ
スクを整理する（資料1）。特にAIが金融市場・システムにもたらす影響を詳しくみていく。

資料 1 GFSR（2026 年 10 月版）があげた三つのリスク

（出所）IMF「GFSR（2026 年 10 月）」より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成 
リスク 評価の変化 評価の概要
AI関連資産
の大幅な値
下がり
横ばい
引き金：AI関連投資の規模や持続性、収益性が期待を下回る。
評価：AI関連資産の規模が拡大し ているため、影響は大きくな
り得る。中核的なAI関連企業の収益は好調で、株価の割高感は
和らいだ一方、AI関連分野でレバレッジが拡大し ている。
国債利回り
の急上昇
悪化
引き金：インフレ再燃、財政問題でタームプレミア ムが上昇。
評価：国債市場は金融市場の基盤で影響は深刻になり得る。高
水準の政府債務や借り換えリスクは未解決のため、財政・イン
フレのショックをより頻繁に受けやすい。
サイバー攻
撃
悪化
引き金：重要な金融機関や市場インフラがサイバー攻撃で被害
評価：デジタル化の急速な進展で攻撃対象が広がり、影響は深
刻になり得る。最先端のAIモデルは攻撃と防御の双方に利用さ
れる可能性。

### Page 2

2/5 
2. AI関連資産の大幅な値下がりリスク  
一つ目のリスクにあげたのはAI関連資産の大幅な値下がりだ。4 月版からの変化を「横ばい」と
評価した。しかし、これは状況が変わっていないという意味ではない。AI関連の中核企業が高い
利益成長を続け、株価の割高感が薄れた点を評価した一方、巨額の設備投資によるフリーキャッ
シュフローの減少や社債発行の増加、投資家によるレバレッジ拡大を警戒している。 
IMFは、AI関連投資を世界経済や金融市場の重要な下支え要因と評価している。生成AIなどの普
及に伴い、データセンターや半導体投資は活発で、関連企業の業績は好調だ。AI関連企業の株価
が急上昇した一方、予想利益も大きく伸びている。GFSRは、一部の株式市場で予想利益の伸びが
株価上昇を上回り、予想株価収益率（PER）が低下した点を評価している。 
一方、リスクが高まっている点として注目するのは、大規模なクラウドサービスやデータセン
ターを運営する企業（ハイパースケーラー）のフリーキャッシュフロー（事業で稼いだ現金から
設備投資などを差し引いた額）の減少だ。設備投資の急増により、資金調達のため社債発行が拡
大している。IMFによると、世界の投資適格社債発行額に占めるハイパースケーラーの割合は、
2024 年の1％未満から2026 年には約10％に上昇した（資料2）。社債の信用スプレッドも、4 月版
GFSRの公表以降、投資適格社債市場全体と比べて約30 ベーシスポイント拡大したという（注 1）。

資料 2 世界の投資適格社債発行額に占めるハイパースケーラーの割合

（出所）IMF「GFSR（2026 年 10 月）」より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

現在のAI関連企業の資金調達の特徴として、資金と売上が企業間で相互に結びつく循環的な取
引があげられる。具体的には、大手企業が財務基盤の相対的に弱いAI企業に出資し、出資先がそ

### Page 3

3/5 
の資金で大手企業のサービスや半導体を購入する仕組みだ。GFSRによれば、2023 年以降に公表さ
れた 14 件の取引では、株式投資が約3,000 億ドル、関連する債務が約6,500 億ドルにのぼり、将
来の売上に関する契約は約1 兆 4,000 億ドルに達する。こうした取引はうまくいけば、出資先の
企業価値拡大による投資リターンの増加と売上増加が期待できる。一方、投資先の経営が悪化す
れば、投資損失と将来の売上減少という逆回転が生じる。IMFは、こうした取引による投資資金や
将来の売上が少数の企業に集中しており、特定の投資先の経営悪化がAI関連企業の間で損失を広
げるリスクを指摘している。 
AI関連企業に投資する側の懸念としてGFSRが取り上げたのは、株価上昇が続くなか、借り入れ
などを利用した取引が拡大し、投資家が過大なリスクを抱えている可能性だ。ヘッジファンドの
株式ポジションと信用取引融資残高の合計は、2024 年末の先進国GDP比約10％から2026 年には約
15％に増加した。また、レバレッジ型上場投資信託（ETF）に占める個別株を対象としたETFの割
合は、16％から28％に上昇した。GFSRの補足分析によると、株式オプションの価格から推計した
レバレッジ取引の資金調達コストも、2026 年初めからほぼ倍増している（注 2）。レバレッジをか
けた取引は株価が下落した際の損失が大きくなり、追加証拠金を拠出するために他の資産を売ら
ざるを得なくなるといった形で、他の金融商品にも波及する場合がある。

3. 国債利回りの急上昇リスク  
AIと同様に金融市場で注目が高まっている国債について、GFSRは利回りの急上昇リスクを4 月
版から「悪化」と評価した。特に注目したいのは、長期国債を保有するリスクに応じて投資家が
求める上乗せ金利（タームプレミアム）の上昇だ。IMFによると、10 年国債利回りの上昇には将来
の政策金利の予想とタームプレミアムの双方が寄与しており、特に先進国のタームプレミアムは
歴史的な変動範囲の上限に近い水準にある。 
タームプレミアムを押し上げているのは、財政赤字の継続や高水準の政府債務に対する懸念、
今後の国債発行増加の見通しだ。ユーロ圏、英国、米国では、2026 年1～3 月期に投資適格社債の
発行が国債を含む債券発行総額の4 割を占めた（デュレーション加重） 。さらにGFSRは、AI関連企
業による社債発行が急増し、国債と投資家の資金を奪い合うことで、国債市場への追加的な圧力
になっているとの指摘も紹介している。 
IMFが警戒するのは、インフレ再燃などをきっかけにタームプレミアムがさらに上昇する事態
だ。主要国では、政府債務が歴史的に高い水準にあるという問題が未解決のままで、国債の借り
換えリスクも高い。特に、国債の償還年限を短期化した国では、金利が上昇すると高い金利で借
り換える必要が生じ、財政負担が増える。長期債から短期債への置き換えが大規模に進めば、こ
うしたリスクは一段と高まる可能性がある。 
政府債務が増えて国債発行が膨らむと、国内の銀行や保険会社などだけでは増発分を消化しき
れず、外国人投資家や投資ファンドの存在感が高まりやすい。こうした投資家は価格変動に敏感
で、インフレ懸念が強まった際に長期国債の売却が集中する可能性がある。GFSRでは、これをタ
ームプレミアムの上昇を増幅する要因としてあげている。

### Page 4

4/5 
GFSRは、日本国債市場の流動性悪化を指摘している。日本銀行によるイールドカーブ・コント
ロールの終了後、金融政策の正常化が進むなかで利回りの変動が大きくなったことに加え、特に
長期国債で売値と買値の差が拡大している点をあげた。ユーロ圏と英国では流動性を示す指標が
改善し、米国でも足元は良好な状況にあるのとは対照的だ。 
IMFはタームプレミアムが急上昇した場合、AI関連株を含む幅広い資産の価格が下落するほか、
金融取引に利用される国債の担保価値も低下し、金融市場全体に混乱が広がる可能性を警戒して
いる。IMFは世界各国の銀行を対象に、国債利回りの急上昇やAIの生産性向上効果が期待を下回る
ことなどを想定したストレステストを実施した。この結果、世界全体の銀行システムの自己資本
比率は規制上の最低水準を上回るものの、ストレス後の普通株式等Tier1 比率が 7％を下回る銀行
の資産は、世界の銀行資産全体の22％を占める結果になった（注3）。

4. サイバー攻撃のリスク 
GFSRは、三つ目のリスクとしてあげたサイバーリスクについて、「悪化」したと評価した。GFSR
では金融機関が外部の情報技術サービスへの依存を強めている点を問題視した。クラウドサービ
スやソフトウエアを提供する企業の一部に利用が集中し、多くの金融機関が共通の技術基盤を利
用するようになっている。こうした企業が攻撃を受けてサービスを停止すれば、金融機関への影
響が広範に及ぶ可能性がある。さらに、外部委託先のつながりが複雑になり、リスクの全体像を
把握しにくくなっている点も指摘した。 
サイバー攻撃は高度化が進み、日本でも企業の情報流出が相次ぐ。GFSRは、最先端のAIモデル
がネットワーク攻撃の模擬実験で高い能力を示していると指摘した。GFSRによると、悪用が確認
されたソフトウエアなどの脆弱性のうち、公表日以前または公表当日に悪用された割合（ゼロデ
イ比率）は、2026 年の年初来で70％を超え、前年から約20 ポイント上昇した。脆弱性が公表さ
れてから悪用されるまでの時間も短くなっており、ソフトウエアの提供企業が修正プログラムを
用意する余裕は小さくなっている。

5. おわりに 
今回のGFSRからは、AIの急成長に金融市場や金融システムがどこまで対応できるのかという問
題意識が垣間見える。AI関連企業の利益成長が世界的な株高をけん引する一方、巨額の設備投資
に必要な資金を調達するため、社債市場でもその存在感を高め始めた。技術革新が進むほど投資
資金への需要も膨らみ、あらゆる市場から資金を吸い上げる構図だ。 
成長分野に資金が集中し、市場が過熱することは過去にも繰り返されてきた。今回のAIブーム
で特徴的なのは、巨額の資金需要が国債市場の需給にまで影響を及ぼす可能性があることだ。AI
の成長が金融市場を動かす一方、金利の上昇が今度はAI投資の採算を左右する。相互依存の強ま
りが今回のGFSRからも確認できたといえる。 
以 上

### Page 5

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
5/5
【注釈】
1) IMF「Global Financial Stability Report」（2026 年 10 月版） 、第1 章補足分析1.1「Rising
Leverage in the AI Ecosystem」3 頁。
2) 上記補足分析1.1、5～6 頁、図 1.1.4。資金調達コストは、S&P500 の株式オプション価格から
推計した担保付翌日物調達金利に対する30 日間の上乗せ金利。
3) IMF「Global Financial Stability Report」（2026 年 10 月版） 、第1 章 7～9 頁、図1.7 および
補足分析1.2「2026 IMF Global Banking Stress Test」。ストレステストの対象は29 カ国の

665 銀行。

## Document 211: CTCP Nhựa Bình Minh - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-23)

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục 
tiêu 166,100 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Doanh thu/LNST 2Q2026 đạt lần lượt 1,320/367 tỷ VND (+1%/+11% YoY). Lũy kế 
6M2026, doanh thu đạt 2,777 tỷ VND (+3% YoY) trong khi LNST ước đạt 671 tỷ VND 
(+9% YoY). Biên lãi gộp 6M2026 cải thiện đạt 47.0% (+240 bps YoY), chủ yếu do việc 
tăng giá bán giúp doanh nghiệp bù đắp được mức tăng của chi phí đầu vào.

Lũy kế 6M2026, sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP ước đạt 43,920 tấn, giảm 5% svck. Việc 
BMP thu hẹp các chương trình chiết khấu, với mức chiết khấu bình quân khoảng 
5.2% trong 2Q2026, làm giảm động lực nhập hàng của các đại lý và khiến sản lượng 
tiêu thụ chậm lại. Dù vậy, thị phần toàn quốc của BMP ước đạt 28% (+1 đpt svck) 
nhờ các doanh nghiệp khác trong ngành gặp khó khăn khi giá hạt nhựa tăng mạnh.

Biên lợi nhuận gộp của BMP dự kiến thu hẹp trong 2H2026 trước áp lực chi phí 
nguyên liệu đầu vào tăng. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi kỳ vọng mức độ ảnh hưởng sẽ thấp 
hơn so với các doanh nghiệp trong ngành do (1) các lô nguyên liệu giá cao từ 
1Q2026 đã được đưa vào sản xuất và tiêu thụ một phần trong 1H2026; (2) BMP chủ 
động nâng gấp đôi hàng tồn kho để ứng phó với biến động bất lợi của giá nguyên 
liệu; (3) hơn 90% nhu cầu nguyên liệu uPVC được cung ứng từ các đối tác trong 
nước như hệ sinh thái SCG tại Việt Nam, AGC Chemicals và LSP; và (4) công ty duy 
trì giá bán bình quân ở mức cao.

Dựa trên kết quả kinh doanh và định giá, chúng tôi khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP với cổ 
phiếu BMP, giá mục tiêu là 166,100 VND/cổ phiếu (tương ứng với mức tăng 14.3% 
so với giá đóng cửa ngày 23/09/2026).

Tăng trưởng doanh thu và lợi nhuận thuần 5 năm gần nhất Hoạt động kinh doanh

Nhựa Bình Minh (BMP) là doanh nghiệp hàng đầu 
trong lĩnh vực sản xuất ống nhựa với thị phần 
dẫn đầu tại thị trường nội địa (27% vào cuối 
2023), thị trường chính tập trung tại khu vực 
miền Nam. BMP có 4 nhà máy với tổng công suất 
đạt 150,000 tấn/năm. 
Nguồn: BMP, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Kỳ vọng đà phục hồi của xây dựng dân dụng và bất động sản miền Nam sẽ thúc đẩy sản lượng tiêu 
thụ tăng trưởng trong 2026–2027. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Giá PVC được kỳ vọng sẽ hạ nhiệt và khả năng giữ giá bán đầu ra ở mức cao giúp BMP giữ biên lợi 
nhuận gộp ở nền cao. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo

### Page 4

4

Nhu cầu nhập hàng thận trọng và 
cắt giảm các đợt chiết khấu trong 
kỳ của BMP làm giảm sản lượng tiêu 
thụ trong 2Q2026

Sự hồi phục của nhu cầu xây dựng 
dân dụng và nguồn cung các dự án 
bất động sản tại miền Nam sẽ thúc 
đẩy tăng trưởng sản lượng 2H2026 
 Sản lượng tiêu thụ trong 2Q2026 ước đạt 20,100 tấn, giảm 11% svck, nâng tổng 
sản lượng tiêu thụ 6M2026 ước đạt 43,920 tấn, giảm 5% svck do các yếu tố bất 
lợi từ bên ngoài như (1) giá hạt nhựa tăng cao khiến thị trường dè chừng trong 
việc nhập hàng vì lo ngại nguồn cung bất ổn (2) BMP đã thu hẹp các chương 
trình chiết khấu so với 2025 ngay trước thời điểm xung đột bùng nổ, giúp tỷ lệ 
chi phí bán hàng/doanh thu về mức 11.3%, giảm 800 bps so với 1Q2026 ở mức 
19.3%, nhưng đồng thời cũng khiến sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP suy giảm do 
chính sách bán hàng thắt chặt hơn với các đối thủ có thị phần thấp hơn.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP dần hồi phục từ 3Q2026 khi nhu 
cầu nhập hàng của các đại lý cải thiện sau giai đoạn thận trọng trong nửa đầu 
năm. Về cuối năm, hoạt động xây dựng dự kiến tăng tốc khi điều kiện thi công 
thuận lợi hơn sau mùa mưa. Bên cạnh đó, việc triển khai các dự án nhà ở tại 
TP.HCM và các tỉnh lân cận được kỳ vọng hỗ trợ nhu cầu ống nhựa trong giai 
đoạn tới. Theo đó, chúng tôi dự phóng sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP lần lượt đạt 
96,695/100,125/96,750 tấn trong 2026/2027/2028.

Biểu đồ 3. Tương quan giữa tỷ lệ chi phí bán hàng/doanh 
thu và lợi nhuận thuần/sản lượng tiêu thụ

Chí Minh

ngành nhờ lợi thế nguồn nguyên liệu và thị

cầu tiêu thụ toàn ngành suy giảm.

### Page 5

5

Lợi thế từ hệ sinh thái công ty mẹ 
SCG và đối tác Nhật Bản giúp BMP 
có nhiều dư địa để nâng cao hiệu 
quả hoạt động

Theo chia sẻ từ ban lãnh đạo, BMP có lợi thế vì nhập nguyên liệu từ công ty mẹ 
là hệ sinh thái SCG tại Việt Nam và đối tác Nhật Bản là AGC Chemicals Vietnam, 
giúp giảm được 5% thuế nhập khẩu và chi phí vận chuyển. Điều này giúp BMP 
hưởng lợi kép khi có thể tăng được giá bán theo thị trường nhưng nguyên vật 
liệu rẻ hơn so với các doanh nghiệp trong ngành. KBSV nhận thấy định hướng 
dài hạn của BMP là cân bằng lợi ích cổ đông và khách hàng, vì vậy việc giảm giá 
bán để giành thị phần không phải lựa chọn ưu tiên của doanh nghiệp mà là 
nâng cao tỷ suất lợi nhuận/sản phẩm thành phẩm.

KBSV dự báo biên lợi nhuận gộp thu hẹp trong 2H2026 khi chi phí nguyên liệu 
giá cao từ 1H2026 được phản ánh vào giá vốn, và hồi phục trong 2027–2028 
nhờ đặc tính giữ giá bán ít thay đổi của nhóm nhựa. Chúng tôi kỳ vọng giá PVC 
resin chịu áp lực giảm từ (1) nhu cầu xây dựng tại Trung Quốc còn yếu khi thị 
trường bất động sản chưa hồi phục rõ ràng; và (2) các nhà sản xuất giải phóng 
hàng tồn kho sau mùa mưa, làm tăng sản lượng hạt nhựa PVC resin. Theo đó, 
KBSV dự phóng biên lợi nhuận gộp của BMP lên 46.4%/46.7%/46.5% trong 
2026/2027/2028.

gộp của BMP

MACROMICRO RESEARCH ｜每⽇財經摘要
全球⼗⼤財經事件｜ 2026/10/13 07:50 （台灣時間）
涵蓋區間：台灣時間  10/10 07:50 ⾄  10/13 07:50 （近 72 ⼩時）。本期⼊選 10 件：近 12 ⼩時 6 件、 12 ⾄
48 ⼩時 3 件、 48 ⾄ 72 ⼩時 1 件。
事件  1
10/12 克⾥夫蘭聯儲總裁哈瑪克：利率需進⼀步上調（台灣時間  10/13 00:44 ）
五⼤重點
1  2026 年 FOMC （聯邦公開市場委員會）票委、克⾥夫蘭聯儲總裁哈瑪克 10/12 接受專訪並公開
發⾔表⽰，利率需要進⼀步上調，⽬前政策的限制性不⾜，通膨回到 2% 的速度過慢。
2   她傾向「幅度較⼩、頻率較⾼」的升息，但未給出下次升息時點；她並表⽰⾃⼰對中性利率的
估計在 FOMC 中偏⾼，若估錯也只可能是低估。
3   她說明理由：企業計畫漲價幅度超過投⼊成本漲幅，是「通膨思維」固化的早期警訊；能源不
是唯⼀因素，強勁需求也在推升價格。

4   她強調殖利率上升「絕對無法」取代升息，並稱⽬前美債殖利率以歷史標準看屬適中；談話當
天美元指數收漲 0.4% ⾄ 102.58 ，美國現貨公債因哥倫布⽇休市。
5   本週將公布美國 9 ⽉ CPI （消費者物價指數），主席沃什預定本週稍晚發⾔， 10 ⽉ FOMC 會議為
10/27 ⾄ 10/28 ；她的談話是會前靜默期前的重要鷹派訊號。
三⼤結論

### Page 2

1   聯準會 9 ⽉升息⾄ 3.75% ⾄ 4.00% 後，鷹派委員仍在推動下⼀步，市場若把 10 ⽉按兵不動解讀
為升息循環結束，⾵險偏⾼；短天期國庫券仍是承接政策不確定性的⾸選。
2   她點出企業漲價預期與需求⾯通膨，代表就業降溫未必⾜以讓聯準會轉向；當 9 ⽉ CPI 的核⼼
服務與能源同步⾛⾼時，應降低⾼估值成⻑股與⻑天期公債部位，保留短券與現⾦。
3   她強調殖利率上升不能取代升息，意味 10 年期殖利率在 5.2% 附近仍可能被政策路徑推升；當
核⼼ PCE 三個⽉年化維持約 2% 且 10 年期殖利率停⽌創⾼時，才逐步把到期短券轉向⾼品質股
票。
事件  2
10/12 布蘭特原油收漲 1.84% ⾄每桶 106.65 美元，胡塞攻擊沙烏地機場與能源設
施（台灣時間  10/13 02:53 ）
五⼤重點
1   布蘭特原油期貨 10/12 收在每桶 106.65 美元，上漲 1.93 美元、漲幅 1.84% ，盤中站上 107 美元，
為逾兩週⾼點； WTI 11 ⽉合約收在每桶 93.74 美元。

2   葉⾨胡塞武裝宣稱 24 ⼩時內對沙烏地發動四波攻擊，命中利雅德哈⽴德國王國際機場及沙烏地
阿美東部的天然氣與油⽥設施；沙國⺠航局延⻑利雅德機場關閉期間。
3   伊朗⾰命衛隊稱兩天內已打擊第三艘試圖駛離霍爾⽊茲海峽的「違規」⼤型油輪，⾄少 10 艘空
載超⼤型油輪在阿聯酋富查伊拉附近停航或掉頭，海峽運輸⾵險再升。
4  OPEC+ 部⻑ 10/12 在利雅德會商，並警告能源運輸受阻已使航運與保險成本升⾄貨值三分之⼀
以上，說明供給衝擊正透過運費與保費推⾼到岸成本。
5   伊朗最⾼國家安全委員會秘書稱，美⽅對伊朗停戰條件的回應「不⾜」，伊朗不會回到先前的
外交路線；談判若無突破，海峽航運恢復時程將再延後。
三⼤結論
1   油價重回百元以上來⾃實體設施與航道受攻擊，屬供給衝擊⽽⾮需求轉強；原油維持事件避險
定位，當運輸恢復、庫存回補時應降低部位，不把短期價格衝擊當成⻑期核⼼資產。
2   當布蘭特持續站穩每桶 100 美元以上且美國核⼼服務價格同步上⾏時，應預期聯準會暫停升息
的定價迅速逆轉，並縮短債券存續期間、降低⾼估值成⻑股。

### Page 3

3   能源類股可作通膨對沖，但應優先低成本、⾼⾃由現⾦流與⾼股息的⼤型業者；第四季油價情
境區間為每桶 80 ⾄ 110 美元，若海峽通航恢復，上緣⾵險下降，能源部位應同步調降。
事件  3
10/12 費城半導體指數跌 2.7% 連四跌，美光跌 3.9% （台灣時間  10/13 04:00 美
股收盤）
五⼤重點
1   費城半導體指數 10/12 下跌 2.7% ，連續第四個交易⽇收⿊，為 7 ⽉以來最⻑連跌；那斯達克跌
0.4% ⾄ 27,245.90 點，道瓊則漲 0.4% ⾄ 51,850.55 點。
2   記憶體股跌幅居前：美光收跌 3.88% ⾄ 989.04 美元，閃迪跌逾 6% ，台積電 ADR 跌 2% 、連五⽇
下跌；同⽇南韓 KOSPI 指數重挫 3.65% ⾄ 6,384.24 點。
3   賣壓主要源於中國⻑鑫存儲的 DRAM 技術進展：其總裁 10/10 表⽰ 4F² 架構研發取得關鍵進展，
預計年底推出整合混合鍵合技術的新⼀代 DDR5 伺服器模組，市場擔憂供給競爭加劇。

4   現貨報價尚未轉弱： DDR5 16Gb 現貨均價 10/12 較前⼀⽇上漲 0.91% ， DDR4 16Gb 上漲
0.29% ，顯⽰這波下跌反映的是競爭預期，⽽⾮報價反轉。
5   光通訊與網通晶⽚同步⾛弱， Lumentum 跌近 10% 、 Semtech 跌 10.15% 、 Marvell 跌 3.42% ，顯
⽰賣壓由記憶體擴散⾄估值較⾼的 AI 硬體族群。
三⼤結論
1   本次下跌的觸發點是競爭者技術進展，⽽⾮記憶體報價或⻑約轉弱；記憶體仍是 AI 供應鏈中議
價⼒最強的環節，但⾼ Beta 個股波動加⼤，應以⿓頭為核⼼、⾼ Beta 為衛星並分批調整。
2   當 DRAM 與 NAND 平均售價先於需求下滑，或⻑約出現減量與延期時，應快速下修記憶體部
位；在報價與位元出貨仍同向成⻑前，股價回檔屬估值重置⽽⾮需求轉折。
3   台積電 ADR 連五⽇下跌，顯⽰資⾦正重新排序⾼估值 AI 硬體；本週台積電與 ASML 第三季財報
是驗證點，當營收、⽑利率與資本⽀出指引同步上修時，才宜回補先進製程與封裝部位。

事件  4
10/12 美元指數升⾄ 102.58 ，創 2025 年 4 ⽉以來新⾼（台灣時間  10/13 04:00 美
股收盤）
五⼤重點

### Page 4

1   美元指數 10/12 上漲 0.4% 收在 102.58 ，盤中⾼點 102.61 ，為 2025 年 4 ⽉以來最⾼；相對過去 52
週低點 95.55 已回升約 7.4% （推算）。
2   主要貨幣同步⾛弱：歐元兌美元約 1.116 、接近 17 個⽉低點，英鎊滑向 1.32 、接近逾三個⽉低
點，⽇圓在每美元 158.5 附近，逼近曾觸發⽇本當局⼲預的 160 關⼝。
3   推升美元的因素包括中東衝突推⾼油價、市場預期美國通膨再升溫，以及聯準會官員偏鷹發
⾔；美國商品期貨交易委員會持倉資料顯⽰，投機性美元多頭出現近期最⼤的三週增幅。
4   哈薩克央⾏副⾏⻑表⽰，美債殖利率處於多年⾼位，使美元⽐⿈⾦更具吸引⼒，該⾏將把⿈⾦
占外匯儲備⽐重降低約 3 個百分點，反映部分央⾏購⾦需求轉弱。
5   ⿈⾦ 10/12 下跌約 0.6% ⾄每盎司約 4,166 美元，較 52 週⾼點 5,586 美元回落約 25% （推算）；本
週美國 9 ⽉ CPI 若⾼於預期，美元與殖利率可能再⾛⾼。

三⼤結論
1   美元強勢來⾃利差與避險，但⻑線仍受美國逾 40 兆美元債務與⻑債供給牽制，不宜視為單向
交易；保留美元短券作為流動性部位，並依實質利差調節避險⽐率。
2   美元升值提⾼台幣投資⼈的海外資產換匯報酬，也壓抑亞幣與外資流⼊；當美元指數續升且台
韓外資持續流出時，應保留美元現⾦與部分避險，降低未避險的集中曝險。
3   ⿈⾦受美元與實質利率壓制，但財政與地緣尾端⾵險仍在；⿈⾦維持 10% ⾄ 15% 分批避險配
置，當實質利率快速上升時暫緩追價，⽽⾮因短線回落全⾯撤出。
事件  5

（台灣時間  10/12 21:51 ）

⾼且持續上升、政治與社會環境使財政整頓困難，以及潛在成⻑偏低三項因素拖累。
2   法國 10 年期公債殖利率 10/12 升⾄約 4.9% ，逼近本⽉稍早創下的 5.03% 、逾⼆⼗年⾼點；法德

10 年期利差約 139.5 個基點。
3   預算草案⽬標是把 2027 年⾚字降⾄ GDP 的 5% ，但國會分裂、撙節措施不受歡迎，社會黨與國
⺠聯盟都反對現⾏⽅案，⾚字⽬標的可信度受到質疑。

### Page 5

4   歐元兌美元 10/12 跌破 1.12 、接近 17 個⽉低點，德國 10 年期公債殖利率則⼩幅降⾄ 3.48% ，資
⾦轉向德債避險，顯⽰壓⼒集中在法國⽽⾮全歐元區。
5   後續關鍵時點為國會預算審議與 10/23 穆迪評等檢視；若預算無法在 12/17 前通過，政府可能以
特別法沿⽤舊預算，財政整頓將再延後。
三⼤結論
1   惠譽點名政治環境使整頓困難，代表法國⾵險已從⾚字數字轉向治理能⼒；應降低法國主權債
存續期間與歐元集中曝險，改以跨國低負債、⾼現⾦流企業與短存續期信⽤資產分散。
2   德債殖利率下滑、法德利差維持⾼檔，顯⽰這是單⼀主權⾵險，不應外推為歐元區普遍違約；
當法國預算通過且法德利差收窄時，才考慮提⾼歐洲週期股⽐重。
3   歐元受法國財政拖累， ECB （歐洲央⾏）升息帶來的利差⽀撐被抵銷；在 10/23 穆迪檢視前，
歐股維持衛星配置，以低負債、⾼現⾦流與國防基建受益者為主。

10/12 Meta 發布 Muse Wonder 模型，祖克柏稱將持續加碼 AI 投⼊（台灣時間

10/13 01:00 ）
五⼤重點
1  Meta 10/12 發布新⼀代 AI 模型 Muse Wonder （內部代號 Watermelon ），開發者可透過 Meta 模型
API 與 Muse Code 使⽤，不久後將⽀援個⼈ AI 助理 Muse 的訂閱版本。
2  Meta 稱該模型具「前沿能⼒」，在程式編寫及⾦融、法律問題等任務可與 OpenAI 、 Anthropic
的頂尖模型競爭； AI 負責⼈ Alexandr Wang 表⽰其在個⼈與專業智慧代理任務達頂尖⽔準。
3   執⾏⻑祖克柏受訪表⽰， Meta 已抵達 AI 前沿，拒絕放緩研發的呼籲，將進⼀步增加⽀出，並稱
公司「已在研發下⼀代更強的模型」。
4  Meta 股價 10/12 收漲 3.38% ⾄ 742.96 美元，盤中⼀度漲 4.47% ；同⽇費城半導體指數跌 2.7% ，
顯⽰資⾦偏好能把 AI 投⼊轉成產品的平台公司，⽽⾮上游硬體。
5  Meta 將於 10/21 公布第三季財報，市場將檢視資本⽀出指引的上調幅度，以及 Muse 訂閱與 API

收⼊能否覆蓋新增折舊。
三⼤結論

### Page 6

1   祖克柏明⾔續增⽀出，⽀持雲端巨頭資本⽀出與 AI 供應鏈訂單能⾒度；但這是資源承諾⽽⾮已
實現收⼊，應以終端變現、 ROIC （投⼊資本報酬率）與⾃由現⾦流作為加碼⾨檻。
2   模型上線並開放 API 與訂閱，代表 Meta 開始把 AI 投⼊轉成可收費產品；當財報顯⽰相關收⼊增
加且⾃由現⾦流未因資本⽀出惡化時，可提⾼雲端與軟體部位，否則維持中性。
3   ⾼利率下股價反應分化，平台公司上漲、上游硬體回檔；配置上維持台積電、先進封裝與液冷
等瓶頸環節為核⼼，對只依賴資本⽀出敘事的標的降低權重。
事件  7
10/12 中國財政部：新能源⾞出⼝退稅率 2027 年 4 ⽉起由 13% 降⾄ 9% （台灣時
間  10/12 17:08 ）
五⼤重點
1   中國財政部 10/12 公告，⾃ 2027 年 4 ⽉ 1 ⽇起，新能源汽⾞增值稅出⼝退稅率由 13% 下調⾄ 9% ，
並取消電⼦菸裝置等產品的增值稅出⼝退稅。
2   上述產品中徵收消費稅者，出⼝消費稅政策不變、續⽤退（免）稅；適⽤稅率以出⼝報關單註

明的出⼝⽇期認定，⾞廠約有五個多⽉緩衝期。
3   退稅率下降 4 個百分點，等同出⼝⾞每單位貨值多承擔約 4% 的增值稅成本（推算），將壓縮以
價格競爭打⼊海外市場的⾞廠⽑利；今年 1 ⽉中國已公告取消光伏產品出⼝退稅。
4   中汽協 10/12 公布 9 ⽉新能源⾞銷量 182.3 萬輛、年增 13.6% ，占新⾞銷量 61.6% ，連續三個⽉⾼
於 60% ；國內滲透率已⾼，出⼝是⾞廠消化產能的重要管道。
5   新稅率 2027 年 4 ⽉才⽣效，⽣效前可能出現提前出⼝，使 2027 年第⼀季出⼝基期墊⾼，後續解
讀中國汽⾞出⼝數據時需留意這項扭曲。
三⼤結論
1   退稅下調壓縮出⼝⾞廠的價格空間，對依賴出⼝消化產能的中國⾞廠屬利空；中國股市維持低
權重、選擇性配置，不因單⼀產業政策調整改變整體判斷。
2   政策⽅向是減少低價出⼝補貼，短期有助緩和貿易摩擦，但中美休戰只延⾄ 2027 年 1 ⽉ 10 ⽇；
當關稅、稀⼟與 H200 晶⽚交貨出現履約變數時，應進⼀步降低中國出⼝導向部位。

3   中國 8 ⽉社會消費品零售僅年增 0.4% ，⾞市依賴補貼與出⼝；當社會零售、⺠間投資與房地產
資⾦同步回升時，才考慮提⾼中國消費相關部位，在此之前只保留⾼技術製造題材。

轉弱；台股維持核⼼配置，不追單⽇突破，採拉回分批，並以內需與⾦融股降低半導體集中度。
2   融資餘額處近⼀年⾼位、外資連賣，若台積電本週財報不如預期，散戶槓桿可能放⼤回檔；當
外資現貨與期貨同步偏空且美元續強時，應先降低槓桿與⾼ Beta 部位。
3   外資匯出與美元⾛強同時出現，新台幣短線偏弱；持有台股者適度分散幣別，搭配美元短券部
分對沖，不以單⼀匯率點位作為進出依據。

10/11 川普宣布俄烏「能源停⽕」，⽩宮稱仍屬潛在協議（台灣時間  10/11
22:17 ）
五⼤重點
1   美國總統川普 10/11 宣布俄烏戰爭「能源停⽕」即刻⽣效，稱雙⽅同意不再破壞彼此能源設
施；⽩宮官員隨後說明，這是指雙⽅停⽌攻擊煉油廠的潛在協議，後續可能延伸⾄電廠與電網。

### Page 8

2   ⽩宮官員也承認⽅案不對稱：烏克蘭只有⼀座屢遭攻擊、未完全運轉的煉油廠，俄羅斯則擁有
規模⼤得多的煉油網路。
3   烏克蘭總統澤倫斯基表⽰對川普的說法感到意外，但若俄⽅同意，烏⽅願停⽌攻擊俄國煉油
廠；他 10/12 再指控俄⽅持續攻擊烏克蘭能源設施，基輔當晚發⽣爆炸。
4   消息⼀度壓低油價， 10/12 亞洲早盤 WTI 跌 2.00% ⾄每桶 89.14 美元、布蘭特跌 1.9% ⾄ 100.72 美
元；但中東攻擊隨後升溫，布蘭特當⽇反⽽收漲 1.84% 。
5   俄羅斯副總理諾⽡克 10/12 表⽰，俄國企業正與買家洽談柴油銷售，呼應川普 10/10 所稱俄⽅將
供應⼤量柴油；若落實，可緩解全球柴油供給緊張。
三⼤結論
1   能源停⽕仍停留在宣⽰階段，雙⽅攻擊未停，油價重新由中東供給⾵險主導；原油維持事件避
險，不因停⽕消息提前⼤幅降低部位。
2   若俄烏能源停⽕落實、俄國柴油出⼝恢復，成品油價可望回落，有助通膨在⾼基期後下降；當
運輸恢復且庫存回補時，應降低原油部位。

3   歐洲是能源停⽕最直接的受益者，但法國財政與能源成本仍壓抑估值；當天然氣與柴油價格同
步回落、歐元區通膨連續下降時，才宜提⾼歐洲週期股⽐重。
事件  10
10/10 中國發改委：加⼤逆週期調節，推動重⼤⼯程儘早開⼯（台灣時間  10/10
19:02 ）
五⼤重點
1   中國國家發改委主任鄭柵潔 10/10 主持⺠營企業座談會表⽰，第四季是鞏固經濟回升的關鍵窗
⼝，將加⼤逆週期調節⼒度，強化存量與增量政策協同。
2   他表⽰將推動「⼗五五」規劃與「六張網」建設實施⽅案中明確的重⼤⼯程儘早開⼯，以釋放
內需潛⼒，並推動科技創新與產業創新深度融合。
3   座談企業涵蓋⽯化、天然氣供應、農業機械與 AI 應⽤，議題聚焦第三季經濟形勢與國際供需變
化，反映能源成本與外需波動是當前政策關注重點。
4   消息帶動 10/12 A 股電網設備族群開⾼、⾦冠電氣漲停，但上證指數當天收跌 0.43% 、創業板指
跌 2.61% ，逾 4,300 檔個股下跌，政策利多未扭轉整體賣壓。

### Page 9

5   國家統計局數據顯⽰， 8 ⽉社會消費品零售年增僅 0.4% ， 1 ⾄ 8 ⽉固定資產投資年減 7.2% ，其中
房地產開發投資年減 19.9% ，內需仍是政策要補的缺⼝。
三⼤結論
1   發改委強調逆週期與重⼤⼯程，屬穩投資⽅向，可⽀撐電網與基建相關族群；但⺠間投資與房
地產仍負成⻑，政策效果偏供給側，中國股市維持低權重、選擇性配置。
2   重⼤⼯程若加速開⼯，對銅與電⼒設備需求有⽀撐，並與全球電網升級趨勢相互加成；當現貨
與庫存結構確認供給缺⼝時，可分批持有銅，避免追逐單次政策⾏情。
3   當 9 ⽉社會零售、⺠間投資與房地產資⾦同步改善時，才考慮提⾼中國內需部位；在此之前，
中國資產僅保留⾼技術製造與政策受惠題材。
待確認
1. 推算值：事件 4 美元指數⾃ 52 週低點回升約 7.4% 、⿈⾦較 52 週⾼點回落約 25% ；事件 7 退稅成本約 4% ；事件 8 台積
電兩⽇外資賣超合計逾 2.1 萬張，均為依公開數字⾃⾏推算。
2. 事件 1 ：克⾥夫蘭聯儲官網截⾄本報告未刊出 10/12 講稿，內容以專訪與公開發⾔的即時紀錄為準；美國 9 ⽉ CPI 與

沃什發⾔的確切⽇期待官⽅⾏事曆確認。
3. 事件 2 ：胡塞武裝對機場與阿美設施的攻擊細節為其單⽅宣稱，沙烏地官⽅未逐項證實；超⼤型油輪停航數量為船
舶追蹤資料，屬單⼀來源。
4. 事件 3 ：⻑鑫存儲技術進展被視為賣壓主因，屬市場解讀；閃迪、 Lumentum 、 Semtech 跌幅為收盤報導數字，未
逐⼀以交易所資料核對。
5. 事件 4 ：投機性美元多頭「近期最⼤三週增幅」為單⼀來源整理；⿈⾦價格各來源介於 4,165.80 ⾄ 4,167.56 美元，
報告取約 4,166 美元。
6. 事件 5 ：惠譽評論全⽂未能取得，僅依其公開摘要；法德利差 139.5 個基點為市場資料，屬單⼀來源。
7. 事件 6 ： Muse Wonder 的能⼒⽐較為 Meta ⾃述，尚無第三⽅評測；祖克柏談話來⾃專訪， Meta 未發布正式資本⽀
出新指引。
8. 與上⼀輪⽐對：上⼀輪 10 件事件均發⽣於 10/8 以前，皆不在本輪 72 ⼩時範圍內，本輪無因重複⽽排除的主題；本
輪油價事件以 10/11 ⾄ 10/12 胡塞攻擊與布蘭特收盤為主體，與上⼀輪「美國財政部制裁 17 艘伊朗相關油輪」（ 10/8 ）

屬不同主題。

## Document 245: Global Strategy Views_ Competition for Capital

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global Strategy Views_ Competition for Capital.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-17
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1. There are two themes dominating our investor conversations: the impact of AI and 
the rise in interest rates. The two issues are linked as demand for capital from both 
the private and public sectors increase. In the private sector a surge in capex 
spending to fund AI infrastructure has eaten into free cash flow and forced 
companies to raise more in debt and equity markets. Meanwhile, government 
borrowing needs have increased as priorities shift towards upgrading critical 
infrastructure, energy security and defense at a time when cyclical inflationary 
pressures driven by higher energy prices are also resulting in higher policy rates. The 
combination has pushed up the cost of capital. As recently as 2022, for example, 
30-year bond yields in Germany and Japan were close to zero (Exhibit 1). A rise in 
yields, together with more uncertainty (over geopolitics and the future impact of AI) 
have, in combination, pushed up the cost of capital.

2. This rise in yields comes after a near-record period of equity outperformance 
relative to bonds over 10-year holding periods (Exhibit 2).

Exhibit 1: Rising yields and uncertainty have increased the cost of capital

As equities have continued to outperform bonds, equity risk premia have fallen back to 
levels last seen in the late 1990s, leaving equity markets more vulnerable to further

lows (Exhibit 3).

3. The impact of rising bond yields, however, depends not only on the level of yields, but 
also on the reasons as well as the speed of the adjustment (Exhibit 4). It is the speed 
issue that has caused the biggest impact in the recent past. As our US strategists note, 
stocks have usually generated positive returns alongside rising interest rates unless the 
pace of rising rates exceeded 2+ standard deviations and this is a pattern that is broadly

5. While US equities have driven the bulk of equity outperformance for much of the 
fifteen years post the financial crisis, the improvement in earnings across regions, and 
the sharper de-rating in the US, has resulted in equity performance being 
broader-based by geography. Since 2025, the US equity market has been the weakest of 
the major regions (Exhibit 6), a turnaround from the dominant trend since the financial 
crisis and increasing investor returns from a more geographically diversified portfolio.

6. Supportive profit growth has been accompanied by positive earnings revisions, with 
2026 and 2027 estimates moving higher across major regions. There are four broad 
areas which have been driving much of this profit growth. First, technology earnings 
continue to be very robust. Second, rising energy prices have pushed up profits in the 
commodity sector. Third, banks have generally enjoyed strong earnings backed by 
positive nominal GDP growth, steep yield curves and strong private sector balance 
sheets. Finally, sectors such as Industrials have benefited from the surge in AI capex 
spending which has spilled over into improved revenues for the ‘pick and shovels’ of AI 
infrastructure. The breadth of the earnings’ growth has also increased the opportunity 
for investors to diversify across sectors as well as countries.

7. While technology profit growth has remained strong, the surge in capex spending 
among the hyperscalers has increasingly eaten through their free cash flow (Exhibit 8) 
prompting companies to look for alternative sources of funding, in the credit and equity 
markets.  The capex spending growth for AA-rated issuers has been substantial: the 65% 
year-over-year growth in Q2 marks the 10th consecutive quarter that aggregate AA 
capex growth exceeds 35%. They have also turned to the convertible bond market 
where volume has also increased year to date reaching $135 billion in the US, with 
AI-related borrowers driving 44% of total issuance, on our estimates. 
Our Credit team raised their full-year US IG gross issuance forecast by $200bn to $2.3 
trillion and AI-related issuers account for a quarter of USD IG gross supply this year, 
pushing overall supply to a year-to-date record.

### Page 7

surge. Globally technology has seen its PE ratio moderate, falling below its 20-year 
median (Exhibit 10). Meanwhile, the spending of these companies has spilled over into 
better earnings and more visible cash flows in many other parts of the economy. 
Healthcare, Consumer Discretionary and Industrials are all more expensive in absolute 
terms than IT, and Industrials has re-rated and now has a PE close to the 90th percentile 
of its 20-year range.

9. For these reasons, we have argued that Technology equities have not experienced a 
classic bubble because valuation levels are much lower than in previous financial 
bubbles. Nevertheless, the risk for investors is less about valuation and more whether 
there is an unsustainable earnings bubble. Bubbles in earnings, rather than valuations, 
have occurred in previous periods.  The Bank sector, for example, briefly became the 
biggest sector in the S&P 500 in the run up to the financial crisis of 2008/09 (Exhibit 11). 
Unlike the technology bubble of the late 1990s, or the Japanese bubble of the late 
1980s, Bank stocks did not experience a major valuation bubble at the time, but the 
surge in earnings that drove the sector’s outperformance proved to be unsustainable.

### Page 8

There are, however, important differences to the Tech sector today. The earnings of the 
banks were boosted by sharply rising leverage and the asset that they were financing – 
US real estate – did experience a valuation bubble. When the housing market collapsed, 
pushing economies into recession, it triggered a sharp fall in underlying earnings for the 
banking sector. There are important differences today. First, technology profits remain 
very robust, and balance sheets are strong overall. Second, interest coverage ratios for 
the aggregate S&P 500 and its median stock, for example, rank in the 99th and 68th 
percentiles relative to the past 20 years. Third, demand for compute is accelerating and 
outstripping supply; Microsoft recently declared their intent to triple data centre 
capacity in 6 years. The bottlenecks are increasingly power, land and data centre 
capacity rather than a lack of compute demand. At our annual Communacopia 
Technology Conference, Nvidia reiterated its 2030 AI TAM outlook to $3tn-$4tn and 
supply constraints continue to be broad based. Nonetheless, any slowdown in profit 
growth, in an environment of a much higher cost of capital, could put downward 
pressure on equity prices, reducing confidence in future cash flows across the ecosystem 
from the hyperscalers to the ‘pick and shovels’ that have been benefiting from the capex 
boom.  
10. Given the near-term risks in bond markets, we remain neutral on equities, alongside 
other asset classes on a 3-month horizon, but continue to be overweight equities over 
12 months. While profit growth is likely to slow from the recent strong pace, ongoing 
growth in nominal GDP should underpin equity markets and the earnings cycle 
propelling equity returns even without any valuation expansion from current levels and 
our economists continue to see a low risk of recession. As our asset allocation team has 
shown, earnings growth and ROE are likely to continue to be the key drivers of equity 
performance relative to bonds, and we expect these fundamentals to remain supportive 
for equity returns. Nevertheless, we continue to recommend well diversified equity 
exposure as opportunities continue to broaden across geographies, sectors and factors.
